Motivation: 高质量、动作对齐的演示数据是扩展灵巧机器人操作基础模型的主要瓶颈。现有机器人无关的人类演示系统（如UMI范式）存在硬件人机工程学不佳、开环工作流和缺乏系统数据混合策略等限制。

Method: 开发XRZero-G0系统：1）配备顶视摄像头和双专用夹爪的虚拟现实界面提高收集效率；2）提出非本体感知数据的闭环收集、检查、训练和评估流程；3）研究机器人无关数据的经验缩放行为和最优混合比例。

Result: 系统实现85%的数据有效性率，建立透明质量控制机制。实验表明，将少量真实机器人数据与大规模机器人无关数据以10:1比例混合，性能可与纯真实机器人数据集媲美，同时将采集成本降低20倍。构建了2000小时机器人无关数据集，实现零样本跨本体迁移到目标物理机器人。

Conclusion: XRZero-G0提供了一种高度可扩展的方法，通过硬件-软件协同设计和优化的数据混合策略，显著降低了高质量机器人操作数据的获取成本，为实现通用真实世界操作奠定了基础。

Abstract: The acquisition of high-quality, action-aligned demonstration data remains a fundamental bottleneck in scaling foundation models for dexterous robot manipulation. Although robot-free human demonstrations (e.g., the UMI paradigm) offer a scalable alternative to traditional teleoperation, current systems are constrained by sub-optimal hardware ergonomics, open-loop workflows, and a lack of systematic data-mixing strategies. To address these limitations, we present XRZero-G0, a hardware-software co-designed system for embodied data collection and policy learning. The system features an ergonomic, virtual reality interface equipped with a top-view camera and dual specialized grippers to directly improve collection efficiency. To ensure dataset reliability, we propose a closed-loop collection, inspection, training, and evaluation pipeline for non-proprioceptive data. This workflow achieves an 85% data validity rate and establishes a transparent mechanism for quality control. Furthermore, we investigate the empirical scaling behaviors and optimal mixing ratios of robot-free data. Extensive experiments indicate that combining a minimal volume of real-robot data with large-scale robot-free data (e.g., a 10:1 ratio) achieves performance comparable to exclusively real-robot datasets, while reducing acquisition costs by a factor of twenty. Utilizing XRZero-G0, we construct a 2,000-hour robot-free dataset that enables zero-shot cross-embodiment transfer to a target physical robot, demonstrating a highly scalable methodology for generalized real-world manipulation.Our project repository: https://github.com/X-Square-Robot/XRZero-G0

</details>


### [39] [Learning Versatile Humanoid Manipulation with Touch Dreaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13015)
*Yaru Niu,Zhenlong Fang,Binghong Chen,Shuai Zhou,Revanth Senthilkumaran,Hao Zhang,Bingqing Chen,Chen Qiu,H. Eric Tseng,Jonathan Francis,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HTD方法，结合鲁棒全身控制、可扩展的人形机器人数据收集和预测性触觉学习，实现真实世界中灵巧的人形机器人操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要全身稳定性、灵巧手部和接触感知能力，但在频繁接触变化的真实世界操作中仍然具有挑战性

Method: 1) 基于RL的全身控制器提供稳定执行；2) VR遥操作+人体运动映射的数据收集系统；3) HTD多模态编码器-解码器Transformer，将触觉作为核心模态，通过行为克隆和触觉梦境训练

Result: 在五个接触丰富的任务中，HTD相比最强基线平均成功率相对提升90.9%；潜在空间触觉预测比原始触觉预测效果更好，成功率相对提升30%

Conclusion: 结合鲁棒全身执行、可扩展的人形机器人数据收集和预测性触觉中心学习，能够实现真实世界中多功能、高灵巧度的人形机器人操作

Abstract: Humanoid robots promise general-purpose assistance, yet real-world humanoid loco-manipulation remains challenging because it requires whole-body stability, dexterous hands, and contact-aware perception under frequent contact changes. In this work, we study dexterous, contact-rich humanoid loco-manipulation. We first develop an RL-based whole-body controller that provides stable lower-body and torso execution during complex manipulation. Built on this controller, we develop a whole-body humanoid data collection system that combines VR-based teleoperation with human-to-humanoid motion mapping, enabling efficient collection of real-world demonstrations. We then propose Humanoid Transformer with Touch Dreaming (HTD), a multimodal encoder--decoder Transformer that models touch as a core modality alongside multi-view vision and proprioception. HTD is trained in a single stage with behavioral cloning augmented by touch dreaming: in addition to predicting action chunks, the policy predicts future hand-joint forces and future tactile latents, encouraging the shared Transformer trunk to learn contact-aware representations for dexterous interaction. Across five contact-rich tasks, Insert-T, Book Organization, Towel Folding, Cat Litter Scooping, and Tea Serving, HTD achieves a 90.9% relative improvement in average success rate over the stronger baseline. Ablation results further show that latent-space tactile prediction is more effective than raw tactile prediction, yielding a 30% relative gain in success rate. These results demonstrate that combining robust whole-body execution, scalable humanoid data collection, and predictive touch-centered learning enables versatile, high-dexterity humanoid manipulation in the real world. Project webpage: humanoid-touch-dream.github.io.

</details>

### [40] [Simple Types for Polymorphic Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12194)
*Barry Jay,Johannes Bader*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出一种简单的组合逻辑类型系统，其中组合子最多只有一个类型，其多态性通过应用来展现。组合类型精确描述其值的结构，支持超越Hindley-Milner系统的多态性和有效的类型推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一个简单但表达能力强的类型系统，能够精确描述组合逻辑中值的结构，支持超越传统Hindley-Milner系统的多态性，同时保持类型推断的有效性和形式化简单性。

Method: 为组合逻辑设计类型系统，其中每个组合子最多只有一个类型，多态性通过应用操作来揭示。组合类型精确描述值的结构，抽象类型（如列表类型和函数类型）可以隐藏这些结构。系统不依赖量化类型，但支持超越Hindley-Milner的多态性。

Result: 提出的类型系统能够精确描述组合逻辑值的结构，支持超越Hindley-Milner系统的多态性，具有有效的类型推断算法。形式化的简单性使得其他静态程序分析更容易进行。

Conclusion: 该类型系统在保持简单性的同时提供了强大的表达能力，支持超越传统系统的多态性和有效的类型推断，为组合逻辑的静态分析提供了更简单的基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces a simple type system for combinatory logic in which combinators have at most one type, whose polymorphism is revealed by application. The combinatory types exactly describe the structure of their values, which may be hidden by abstract types, such as list types and function types. Even without any quantified types, it supports polymorphism beyond that of the Hindley-Milner type system that underpins functional programming, and an effective type inference algorithm. Also, the simplicity of the formalism should make other static program analyses easier.

</details>


### [41] [Recursive Completion in Higher K-Models: Front-Seed Semantics, Proof-Relevant Witnesses, and the K-Infinity Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12981)
*Daniel O. Martinez-Rivillas,Arthur F. Ramos,Ruy J. G. B. de Queiroz*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文证明较小的前种子相干包(WL, WR)结合内右前五边形收缩足以恢复关联子比较、语义五边形和桥定理，并给出K-无穷的显式全局重化、反射和应用公式的精确坐标恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Martinez-Rivillas和de Queiroz为无类型λ演算提供了外延Kan语义并构建了具体的K-无穷同伦模型。本文旨在简化相干条件并给出K-无穷的显式计算公式。

Method: 使用较小的前种子相干包(WL, WR)结合内右前五边形收缩来恢复语义结构；通过有限打包阶段和均匀等式生成递归来构造递归全维延拓；在固定前向见证语言上分析规范恒等类型高塔。

Result: 1. 较小的相干包足以恢复关联子比较、语义五边形和桥定理；2. 证明了K-无穷的显式全局重化、反射和应用公式的精确坐标恒等式；3. 阐明了递归构造的结构特征和恒等类型高塔的分离性质。

Conclusion: 本文简化了K-无穷同伦模型的相干条件，提供了显式计算工具，并在Lean 4中完全形式化验证，为高阶范畴理论和同伦类型论提供了新的技术基础。

Abstract: Martinez-Rivillas and de Queiroz gave extensional Kan semantics for the untyped lambda-calculus and later constructed the concrete K-infinity homotopy-model. The two main mathematical results of the present paper are these. First, we show that a smaller front-seed coherence package (WL, WR) together with an inner-right-front pentagon contraction already suffices to recover the associator comparison, semantic pentagon, and bridge theorems used in the later semantic arguments. Second, we prove explicit global reify, reflect, and application formulas for K-infinity, with exact coordinatewise identities at every finite stage. We also record two structural clarifications: the recursive all-dimensional continuation of the explicit low-dimensional tower is obtained by a finite packaging phase followed by a uniform equality-generated recursion; and, on a deliberately fixed forward witness language for the classical separation span, the canonical identity-type higher tower on K-infinity forces all higher non-connection once the two witness classes land at distinct points. The paper is fully formalized in Lean 4, and the project sources contain no local uses of sorry, admit, or axiom.

</details>

### [42] [When Drawing Is Not Enough: Exploring Spontaneous Speech with Sketch for Intent Alignment in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11964)
*Weiyan Shi,Dorien Herremans,Kenny Tsu Wei Choo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TalkSketchD数据集捕捉早期设计构思中手绘草图与同步语音，研究显示结合语音能显著提升多模态大模型对设计意图的理解和图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期设计构思通常依赖快速草图，但设计师意图往往隐含在草图中。设计师在绘制草图时经常同时口头表达功能目标和想法，这些难以通过视觉方式完全传达。现有方法主要关注草图本身，忽略了同步语音中蕴含的丰富设计意图信息。

Method: 1. 创建TalkSketchD数据集：捕捉早期烤面包机设计构思过程中的手绘草图与同步语音，确保时间对齐；2. 进行草图到图像生成研究：比较仅使用草图输入与结合语音转录的多模态大模型（MLLMs）生成效果；3. 使用推理MLLM作为评估者，将生成图像与设计师自我报告的设计意图进行对比评估。

Result: 定量结果显示，结合自发语音能显著提高生成设计图像在形式、功能、体验和整体意图方面的对齐度。多模态大模型通过同时处理草图和时间对齐的语音，能更准确地解释用户意图，提升早期设计构思阶段的设计生成质量。

Conclusion: 时间对齐的草图-语音数据能有效增强多模态大模型在早期设计构思中解释用户意图的能力。自发语音提供了补充的语义信息，帮助模型更好地理解设计师的完整意图，为设计辅助工具开发提供了新方向。

Abstract: Early-stage design ideation often relies on rough sketches created under time pressure, leaving much of the designer's intent implicit. In practice, designers frequently speak while sketching, verbally articulating functional goals and ideas that are difficult to express visually. We introduce TalkSketchD, a sketch-while-speaking dataset that captures spontaneous speech temporally aligned with freehand sketches during early-stage toaster ideation. To examine the dataset's value, we conduct a sketch-to-image generation study comparing sketch-only inputs with sketches augmented by concurrent speech transcripts using multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Generated images are evaluated against designers' self-reported intent using a reasoning MLLM as a judge. Quantitative results show that incorporating spontaneous speech significantly improves judged intent alignment of generated design images across form, function, experience, and overall intent. These findings demonstrate that temporally aligned sketch-and-speech data can enhance MLLMs' ability to interpret user intent in early-stage design ideation.

</details>


### [43] [Socially Fluent, Socially Awkward: Artificial Intelligence Relational Talk Backfires in Commercial Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12206)
*Stephanie Kwari Dharmaputri,Anish Nagpal,Greg Nyilasy,Jing Lei*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现AI在商业互动中的社交性对话（关系性谈话）会降低消费者满意度，这种负面效应通过期望违背和感知互动尴尬来中介，但目标相关的关系性谈话可以缓解这种效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI社交流畅性技术被整合到Shopify、Klarna、Visa等商业平台，理解消费者对AI社交功能的反应变得至关重要。需要研究AI关系性谈话（非正式、非义务性的社交沟通）对消费者满意度的影响。

Method: 通过四个实验研究AI关系性谈话对消费者满意度的影响机制。考察期望违背和感知互动尴尬的中介作用，以及目标相关性对负面效应的调节作用。

Result: 1) AI关系性谈话对满意度有负面主效应，通过期望违背和感知互动尴尬中介；2) 目标相关的关系性谈话能够减弱这种负面效应。即使没有真实社交后果，AI主导的商业互动中的感知尴尬也能引发负面反应。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了"增加社交流畅性会提高满意度"的假设，强调了将社交功能整合到AI系统中的复杂性。尴尬被识别为关键情感反应和有效人机互动的障碍，表明即使没有真实社交后果，AI商业互动中的感知尴尬也能引发负面反应。

Abstract: Advancements in Artificial Intelligence (AI) technologies' social fluency are being integrated into commercial interactions. As tools such as OpenAI's assistant are integrated into platforms such as Shopify, Klarna, and Visa, understanding consumer responses to AI social features become essential. One such feature is relational talk, an informal and non-obligatory social communication embedded in transactional exchanges. Across four experiments, we find: 1) a negative main effect of AI relational talk on satisfaction, mediated by expectancy violation and perceived interaction awkwardness, and 2) goal-relevant relational talk to attenuate this effect. This paper extends the literature by challenging the assumption that increased social fluency will improve satisfaction, and highlights the complexity of integrating social features into AI systems. It also identifies awkwardness as a key emotional response and barrier to effective human-AI interaction, showing that even in the absence of real social repercussions, perceived awkwardness in AI-led commercial interactions can elicit negative responses.

</details>


### [44] [Responsible Trauma Research: Designing Effective and Sustainable Virtual Reality Exposure Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12349)
*Annalisa Degenhard,Sophia Ppali,Fotis Liarokapis,Enrico Rukzio,Jennifer Spohrs,Stefan Tschoeke*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VRET用于复杂创伤后应激障碍的可行性研究，发现简单对象与复杂场景同样有效，治疗成功与VR临场感无关，设计过程成为治疗本身，但开发者参与带来情感压力和角色混淆


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟现实暴露疗法（VRET）能够控制性地暴露于创伤相关刺激以促进记忆访问和情感处理，但该领域在复杂创伤后应激障碍（C-PTSD）方面仍未被充分探索。与单一创伤的PTSD不同，C-PTSD需要高度个性化的触发因素，这些因素难以安全地识别和实施。

Method: 进行了可行性研究，涉及11名患者、两名创伤治疗师和一名VR开发者，探索将VRET整合到C-PTSD治疗中，同时保护所有利益相关者的安全。

Result: 初步发现表明：简单对象与复杂场景同样有效；治疗成功与VR临场感水平无关；设计过程本身成为治疗的一部分而非准备阶段。然而，让开发者参与治疗会话导致了相当大的情感压力和角色混淆，需要谨慎处理。

Conclusion: 基于这些见解，提供了安全且以患者为中心的VRET研究的方法学建议，在整个研究过程中平衡治疗效果与利益相关者安全。

Abstract: Virtual reality exposure therapy (VRET) enables controlled exposure to trauma-related stimuli to facilitate memory access and emotional processing. However, the field remains underexplored for complex post-traumatic stress disorder (C-PTSD). Unlike single-trauma PTSD, C-PTSD requires highly individualized triggers that are difficult to identify and implement safely. We conducted a feasibility study with 11 patients, two trauma therapists, and a VR developer to explore integrating VRET into C-PTSD treatment while safeguarding all stakeholders. Initial findings indicate that simple objects can be just as effective as complex scenes, therapeutic success does not correlate with VR presence levels, and the design process itself became integral to therapy rather than preparatory. However, involving developers in therapy sessions led to considerable emotional stress and role confusion, which required a cautious approach. Based on these insights, we provide methodological recommendations for safe and patient-centered VRET studies that balance therapeutic effectiveness with stakeholder safety across the research process.

</details>


### [45] [GraphTide: Augmenting Knowledge-Intensive Text with Progressive Nested Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12624)
*Xin Qian,Dazhen Deng,Zhaoping He,Xingbo Wang,Yuchen He,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GraphTide：一种通过渐进式构建嵌套实体关系图并配以动画的可视化技术，用于支持复杂知识密集型文本的理解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识密集型文本（如学术文章和科学论述）通常包含丰富的实体和复杂的关系，阅读和理解这类文本需要大量时间和精力来追踪实体间的关系。为了减轻这种负担，需要一种可视化技术来支持复杂文本的理解。

Method: 1. 按需实体关系分解管道，构建嵌套图来表示句内和句间关系；2. 结构感知的力导向布局优化算法，增强结构清晰度；3. 通过动画过渡逐步揭示句子及其相关实体，帮助用户在叙述展开时保持上下文。

Result: 用户研究表明，与传统基于图的技术和静态嵌套图表示相比，GraphTide显著提高了用户对知识密集型文本的理解能力。

Conclusion: GraphTide通过渐进式构建嵌套实体关系图并配以动画，有效支持了复杂知识密集型文本的理解，显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Knowledge-intensive text usually contains fruitful entities and complex relationships, such as academic articles and scientific exposition. Reading and comprehending such texts often demands considerable time and mental effort to track the relationships between entities. To reduce the burden, we present GraphTide, a visualization technique that progressively constructs nested entity-relationship graphs with animation to support the understanding of complex text. Our method features an on-demand entity-relationship decomposition pipeline that constructs nested graphs to represent intra- and inter-sentence relationships. Moreover, we propose a structure-aware force-directed layout optimization algorithm to enhance structural clarity. Sentences and their associated entities are incrementally revealed through animated transitions, helping users maintain context as the narrative unfolds. A user study shows that GraphTide significantly improves users' comprehension of knowledge-intensive texts compared to traditional graph-based techniques and static nested graph representations.

</details>


### [46] [A sequential explanatory mixed-methods study on the acceptance of a social robot for EFL speaking practice among Chinese primary school students: Insights from the Computers Are Social Actors (CASA) paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12789)
*Yiran Du,Jinlong Li,Huimin He,Chenghao Wang,Bin Zou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过混合方法探讨中国小学生对英语口语练习社交机器人的接受度，发现感知乐趣和易用性是最强预测因素，社交属性显著增强乐趣体验


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索中国小学生对英语作为外语(EFL)口语练习社交机器人的接受度，整合技术接受模型(TAM)和计算机是社会行动者(CASA)范式，研究功能性和社会性因素如何影响学习者的使用行为意向

Method: 采用顺序解释性混合方法设计：1) 定量阶段：436名学生问卷调查，使用结构方程模型分析；2) 定性阶段：12名学生访谈，解释定量发现。整合TAM和CASA理论框架

Result: 感知乐趣和易用性是最强的接受度预测因素；社交属性(温暖感、拟人化、社会临场感)显著增强乐趣体验；感知智能影响有用性但不影响易用性

Conclusion: 情感和社会参与是年轻学习者接受教育机器人的核心因素，强调设计促进动机和口语自信的社会智能技术的重要性，为EFL学习环境中的机器人设计提供实践启示

Abstract: This study investigates Chinese primary school students' acceptance of a social robot for English-as-a-foreign-language (EFL) speaking practice through a sequential explanatory mixed-methods design. Integrating the Technology Acceptance Model (TAM) and the Computers Are Social Actors (CASA) paradigm, the research explores both functional and social factors influencing learners' behavioural intention to use the robot. Quantitative data from 436 students were analysed using structural equation modelling, followed by qualitative interviews with twelve students to interpret the findings. Results show that perceived enjoyment and ease of use are the strongest predictors of acceptance, while social attributes such as warmth, anthropomorphism, and social presence significantly enhance enjoyment. Perceived intelligence affects usefulness but not ease of use. The findings suggest that emotional and social engagement are central to young learners' acceptance of educational robots, highlighting the importance of designing socially intelligent technologies that promote motivation and speaking confidence in EFL learning contexts.

</details>


### [47] [Human Agency, Causality, and the Human Computer Interface in High-Stakes Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12793)
*Georges Hattab*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文批判当前AI伦理讨论过于关注"可信"和"负责任"AI，而忽视了更根本的人类能动性危机，提出AI应被视为"坏UI"，主张通过因果-能动性框架恢复人类控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI伦理讨论被"可信"和"负责任"AI话语主导，忽视了高风险AI系统中人类能动性被侵蚀的根本问题。作者认为主要挑战不是信任，而是保持人类因果控制。

Method: 应用麦克卢汉媒介理论，将AI视为同时"增强"和"截肢"的技术；批判当前可解释AI的局限性；提出嵌套的因果-能动性框架，整合因果模型、不确定性量化和人本评估。

Result: 建立了将AI系统视为"坏UI"的理论框架，识别了"双重不确定性"问题，提出了恢复人类能动性的方法论路径。

Conclusion: AI伦理的核心应是恢复人类能动性而非建立信任，需要通过因果-能动性框架在界面层面解决双重不确定性，实现人类因果控制的保留。

Abstract: Current discourse on Artificial Intelligence (AI) ethics, dominated by "trustworthy" and "responsible" AI, overlooks a more fundamental human-computer interaction (HCI) crisis: the erosion of human agency. This paper argues that the primary challenge of high-stakes AI systems is not trust, but the preservation of human causal control. We posit that "bad AI" will function as "bad UI," a metaphor for catastrophic interface failures that misrepresent system state and lead to human error. Applying Marshall McLuhan's media theory, AI can be framed as a technology of "augmentation" that simultaneously "amputates" the user's direct perception of causality. This places the interface as the critical locus where a "double uncertainty"--that of the human user and that of the probabilistic model--must be mediated. We critique current Explainable AI (XAI) for its correlational focus and failure to represent uncertainty. We conclude by proposing a rigorous, nested Causal-Agency Framework (CAF) that integrates causal models, uncertainty quantification, and human-centered evaluation to restore agency at the interface.

</details>


### [48] [GlintMarkers: Spatial Perception on XR Eyewear using Corneal Reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12949)
*Seungjoo Lee,Vimal Mollyn,Chris Harrison,Justin Chan,Mayank Goel*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GlintMarkers：首个利用XR眼镜内置摄像头实现注视驱动的空间感知系统，通过角膜反射提取环境空间和语义信息


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XR设备缺乏有效的空间感知能力，需要开发一种利用现有硬件（内置摄像头）实现环境感知的方法，而不增加额外传感器负担

Method: 1. 利用角膜作为镜面反射环境信息的特性；2. 设计被动式逆反射标记，将近红外光集中反射到角膜上产生明亮光斑；3. 开发定制化的PnP估计框架适应角膜成像；4. 实现带标记物体的方向、距离估计和唯一识别

Result: 成功实现了基于注视的空间感知系统，能够从角膜反射中提取环境信息，完成物体定位和识别，解决了摄像头像素预算有限条件下低对比度反射的挑战

Conclusion: GlintMarkers证明了利用XR眼镜内置摄像头和角膜反射实现空间感知的可行性，为轻量级环境感知提供了新途径，无需额外传感器

Abstract: We present GlintMarkers, the first system to perform gaze-driven spatial perception using the inward-facing cameras on XR eyewear. Our key observation is that the cornea acts as a mirror that encodes both gaze direction and visual information about the environment in a small, low-contrast reflection. To extract spatial and semantic information from this reflection despite the camera's limited pixel budget, we present a passive retroreflective marker design that concentrates reflected near-infrared light onto the cornea, producing bright glint patterns. We develop a custom Perspective-n-Point (PnP) estimation framework adapted to corneal imaging and perform orientation and distance estimation of tagged objects, as well as unique object identification.

</details>

### [49] [A Geometric Algebra-informed NeRF Framework for Generalizable Wireless Channel Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11983)
*Jingzhou Shen,Luis Lago Enamorado,Shiwen Mao,Xuyu Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: GAI-NeRF：一种结合几何代数注意力机制和神经辐射场的新型无线信道预测框架，通过全局令牌表示和新型光线追踪架构，在复杂传播环境中实现高效泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态光线追踪模块存在泛化能力不足的局限性，难以适应复杂多变的无线传播环境。需要开发能够有效捕捉射线-物体相互作用并增强场景理解的新方法，以提升无线信道预测性能。

Method: 提出几何代数感知神经辐射场（GAI-NeRF），结合几何代数注意力机制捕捉复杂传播环境中的射线-物体相互作用。引入受Transformer启发的全局令牌表示来聚合学习到的空间-电磁特征。设计新型光线追踪架构以克服传统静态模块的泛化限制。

Result: GAI-NeRF在无线信道预测任务中表现出优越性能，在多个无线场景下大幅超越现有方法。在新收集的真实室内数据集上验证了其鲁棒性，在未见环境中保持高效性能，计算效率良好。

Conclusion: GAI-NeRF通过结合几何代数原理与神经场景表示，为下一代无线通信系统提供了有前景的方向，实现了跨场景的有效泛化，解决了传统方法的局限性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the geometric algebra-informed neural radiance fields (GAI-NeRF), a novel framework for wireless channel prediction that leverages geometric algebra attention mechanisms to capture ray-object interactions in complex propagation environments. Our approach incorporates global token representations, drawing inspiration from transformer architectures in language and vision domains, to aggregate learned spatial-electromagnetic features and enhance scene understanding. We identify limitations in conventional static ray tracing modules that hinder model generalization and address this challenge through a new ray tracing architecture. This design enables effective generalization across diverse wireless scenarios while maintaining computational efficiency. Experimental results demonstrate that GAI-NeRF achieves superior performance in channel prediction tasks by combining geometric algebra principles with neural scene representations, offering a promising direction for next-generation wireless communication systems. Moreover, GAI-NeRF greatly outperforms existing methods across multiple wireless scenarios. To ensure comprehensive assessment, we further evaluate our approach against multiple benchmarks using newly collected real-world indoor datasets tailored for single-scene downstream tasks and generalization testing, confirming its robust performance in unseen environments and establishing its high efficacy for wireless channel prediction.

</details>


### [50] [BLAST: Blockchain-based LLM-powered Agentic Spectrum Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12127)
*Anas Abognah,Otman Basir*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: BLAST框架整合LLM智能体与许可区块链，实现自主、隐私保护的动态频谱交易，通过三种拍卖机制验证Vickrey拍卖最优，LLM智能体显著提升市场效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态频谱管理缺乏去中心化、自动化、可信且保护隐私的协调基础设施，阻碍了从静态集中式管理向动态市场驱动模式的转变。

Method: 提出BLAST框架，整合LLM智能体与许可区块链，采用认知无线电循环（感知-规划-执行）的智能体架构，实现战略经济推理，并评估直接销售、首价密封投标和次价密封投标三种市场机制。

Result: 次价密封投标（Vickrey）拍卖在最大化社会福利和分配效率方面最优，可捕获高达71%的理论剩余；LLM智能体相比非LLM启发式模型显著提升市场竞争、减少财富和资产集中度、增加系统福利；系统有效保护隐私，敏感投标值隔离在私有数据集合中。

Conclusion: BLAST框架成功解决了动态频谱管理的关键挑战，为去中心化、隐私保护的频谱交易提供了可行方案，LLM智能体与区块链的结合为未来频谱市场基础设施提供了新范式。

Abstract: The management of radio frequency spectrum is undergoing a paradigm shift from static, centralized command-and-control models to dynamic, market-driven approaches. However, the realization of Dynamic Spectrum Management has been hindered by the lack of an automated, trustworthy, and intelligent coordination infrastructure that can operate without a central authority while preserving participant privacy. In this paper, we introduce BLAST (Blockchain-based LLM-powered Agentic Spectrum Trading), a comprehensive framework that integrates Large Language Model (LLM) Agents with a permissioned blockchain infrastructure to create a fully autonomous, private, and secure spectrum trading ecosystem. We propose a novel agent architecture that implements the Cognitive Radio cycle through a sequential decision pipeline (perceive, plan, act) enabling agents to reason strategically about economic value and market dynamics. We evaluate the framework through three distinct market mechanisms: Direct Sale, First-Price Sealed-Bid, and Second-Price (Vickrey) Sealed-Bid auctions. Experimental results demonstrate that the Second-Price (Vickrey) auction is the optimal choice for maximizing social welfare and allocative efficiency, capturing up to 71% of the theoretical surplus by incentivizing truthful bidding. We also compare the proposed model against a baseline non-LLM heuristic agentic model and show that utilizing LLM agents yields significant improvements in market competition, reduced wealth and asset concentration, and increased system welfare. Furthermore, we validate the system's privacy preservation, confirming that sensitive bid values remain isolated in private data collections while only cryptographic hashes are committed to the public ledger.

</details>


### [51] [LightTune: Lightweight Forward-Only Online Fine-Tuning with Applications to Link Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12406)
*Ramy E. Ali,Federico Penna*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: LightTune：一种轻量级、无需反向传播的在线微调框架，用于移动设备上的机器学习模型自适应，在6G BLER预测中显著降低误差并提升吞吐量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备部署机器学习算法面临动态真实环境与离线训练条件差异导致的性能下降问题。传统在线学习方法计算成本过高，不适用于资源受限设备，需要轻量级自适应解决方案。

Method: 提出LightTune框架，采用无反向传播的在线微调方法，仅在性能低于预设阈值时利用实时测试数据优化模型，确保最小计算开销和高响应效率。框架具有可证明的收敛保证。

Result: 将LightTune集成到6G系统的块错误率预测算法中，在线微调使平均BLER预测误差降低高达48.8%。基于此预测的链路自适应相比传统OLLA算法，平均吞吐量提升高达15.5%。

Conclusion: LightTune为资源受限移动设备提供了高效、轻量级的在线自适应解决方案，在6G通信系统中显著提升了机器学习模型的实时适应能力和系统性能。

Abstract: Deploying machine learning (ML) algorithms on mobile phones is bottlenecked by performance degradation under dynamic, real-world conditions that differ from the offline training conditions. While continual learning and adaptation are essential to mitigate this distributional shift, conventional online learning methods are often computationally prohibitive for resource-constrained devices. In this paper, we propose LightTune, a lightweight, backpropagation-free online fine-tuning framework with provable convergence guarantees. LightTune opportunistically refines ML models using live test-time data only when performance falls below a predefined threshold, ensuring minimal computational overhead and highly efficient responsiveness.
  As a practical demonstration, we integrate LightTune into a block error rate (BLER) prediction algorithm for 6G mobile systems. This integration enables the ML BLER prediction model to dynamically adapt to previously unseen channel conditions in real-time. Our extensive results show a substantial reduction in the average BLER prediction error of up to 48.8% with online fine-tuning. Furthermore, we leverage this BLER prediction algorithm for link adaptation and demonstrate average throughput improvements of up to 15.5% compared to a conventional table-based outer loop link adaptation (OLLA) algorithm.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [52] [Robust Reasoning and Learning with Brain-Inspired Representations under Hardware-Induced Nonlinearities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12079)
*William Youngwoo Chung,Hamza Errahmouni Barkam,Tamoghno Das,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出基于超维计算的硬件感知优化框架，补偿存内计算中的非线性失真和可靠性问题，显著提升分类和推理任务在非理想硬件下的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习依赖高精度计算和理想硬件假设，而存内计算架构虽然缓解了数据移动瓶颈并提高能效，但引入了非线性失真和可靠性问题。需要一种方法来补偿存内计算中的非理想相似度计算。

Method: 基于超维计算的硬件感知优化框架，将编码制定为优化问题，最小化理想核与其硬件约束对应物之间的Frobenius范数，采用联合优化策略对超向量表示进行端到端校准。

Result: 在严重硬件扰动下，应用于QuantHD的方法达到84%准确率，比朴素QuantHD提高48%；在Cora数据集上，RelHD的准确性得以保持，比朴素RelHD在非线性环境下提高5.4倍准确率。

Conclusion: 该框架通过保持超维计算的鲁棒性和符号特性，实现了可扩展、高能效的智能系统，能够在新兴存内计算硬件上执行分类和推理任务。

Abstract: Traditional machine learning depends on high-precision arithmetic and near-ideal hardware assumptions, which is increasingly challenged by variability in aggressively scaled semiconductor devices. Compute-in-memory (CIM) architectures alleviate data-movement bottlenecks and improve energy efficiency yet introduce nonlinear distortions and reliability concerns. We address these issues with a hardware-aware optimization framework based on Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC), systematically compensating for non-ideal similarity computations in CIM. Our approach formulates encoding as an optimization problem, minimizing the Frobenius norm between an ideal kernel and its hardware-constrained counterpart, and employs a joint optimization strategy for end-to-end calibration of hypervector representations. Experimental results demonstrate that our method when applied to QuantHD achieves 84\% accuracy under severe hardware-induced perturbations, a 48\% increase over naive QuantHD under the same conditions. Additionally, our optimization is vital for graph-based HDC reliant on precise variable-binding for interpretable reasoning. Our framework preserves the accuracy of RelHD on the Cora dataset, achieving a 5.4$\times$ accuracy improvement over naive RelHD under nonlinear environments. By preserving HDC's robustness and symbolic properties, our solution enables scalable, energy-efficient intelligent systems capable of classification and reasoning on emerging CIM hardware.

</details>


### [53] [LightMat-HP: A Photonic-Electronic System for Accelerating General Matrix Multiplication With Configurable Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12278)
*Hailong Gong,Haibo Zhang,Amanda S. Barnard,Mahbub Hassan,Matt Woolley,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: LightMat-HP是一个混合光电计算系统，采用分块浮点算术和切片式光子乘法方案，实现了可配置精度的矩阵乘法加速，在吞吐量、延迟和能效方面优于FPGA、GPU和现有光子加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电子加速器的矩阵乘法性能受限于内存带宽和能效，而现有光子计算系统由于模拟光学调制约束和噪声积累，通常仅限于低精度计算，限制了其在精度关键工作负载中的应用。

Method: 提出LightMat-HP混合光电计算系统：1) 采用分块浮点算术降低计算复杂度并支持灵活的精度-性能权衡；2) 提出基于切片的光子乘法方案，利用低比特宽度光子乘法的高精度结合数字累加实现高精度尾数乘法；3) 设计基于分块的矩阵乘法数据流以支持任意大小的矩阵。

Result: 在光子计算原型机上实验验证，并通过大规模仿真评估性能。结果表明，LightMat-HP在吞吐量、延迟和能效方面优于FPGA、GPU和现有最先进的光子加速器，特别是在中小型矩阵乘法中表现突出，这得益于其高度并行的光子架构、高效的数据移动和基于切片的分块浮点算术。

Conclusion: LightMat-HP成功解决了光子计算系统的精度限制问题，通过混合光电架构和创新的计算方案，实现了可配置精度的高性能矩阵乘法加速，为大规模人工智能和科学计算提供了有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Matrix multiplication is a fundamental kernel in large-scale artificial intelligence and scientific computing, but its performance on conventional electronic accelerators is increasingly constrained by memory bandwidth and energy efficiency. Photonic computing offers a promising alternative due to its ultra-high bandwidth, massive parallelism, and low power dissipation. However, most existing photonic systems are limited to low-precision computation because of analog optical modulation constraints and noise accumulation, which restricts their applicability in precision-critical workloads. To address this limitation, we propose LightMat-HP, a hybrid photonic-electronic computing system that enables end-to-end acceleration of general matrix multiplication with configurable computational precision. LightMat-HP adopts block floating-point (BFP) arithmetic to reduce computational complexity while enabling flexible precision-performance tradeoffs. To overcome the precision limitations of photonic devices, we propose a slicing-based photonic multiplication scheme that exploits the high accuracy of low bit-width photonic multiplication in combination with digital accumulation to achieve high-precision mantissa multiplication. A tile-based matrix multiplication dataflow is further designed to support matrices of arbitrary sizes. We experimentally validate LightMat-HP on a photonic computing prototype and evaluate its performance through large-scale simulations. The results demonstrate that LightMat-HP outperforms FPGA, GPU, and a state-of-the-art photonic accelerator across throughput, latency, and energy efficiency, particularly for small- and medium-sized matrix multiplications, owing to its highly parallel photonic architecture, efficient data movement, and slice-based BFP arithmetic.

</details>

### [54] [Stability and Geometry of Attractors in Neural Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12720)
*Mia-Katrin Kvalsund,James Stovold*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文对神经元胞自动机（NCA）的吸引子动力学进行了首次系统性分析，挑战了NCA学习固定点吸引子的传统观点，揭示了其振荡、周期和准周期行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NCA研究通常假设系统学习的是吸引子，但缺乏对吸引子性质的深入分析。论文旨在回答：吸引子类型是什么？行为是有序还是混沌的？长期稳定性如何？扰动下会发生什么？这些问题需要通过动力系统理论的方法来探究。

Method: 以Mordvintsev等人（2020）的确定性细胞更新gecko NCA为案例研究，采用动力系统理论方法，包括李雅普诺夫谱和傅里叶谱分析，首次可视化NCA吸引子动力学。

Result: 分析显示NCA表现出振荡、周期和准周期行为，这些行为在训练早期就出现，挑战了NCA学习固定点吸引子的传统观点。大扰动可将NCA推入与原始吸引子分离的次级模式。

Conclusion: 该研究为NCA吸引子动力学提供了首个系统性分析框架，扩展了NCA研究者分析系统鲁棒性和稳定性的工具集，揭示了NCA行为的复杂性远超固定点吸引子假设。

Abstract: Throughout the literature on Neural Cellular Automata (NCAs), it is often taken for granted that the systems learn attractors. This is shown through evolving the system for many timesteps and noting visual similarity to the goal state. There remain many questions after such an analysis. Namely, what kind of attractors do we have? Is their behavior ordered or chaotic? Can we estimate stability over very long time horizons? What really happens in the attractor when perturbations are applied? In this paper, we present a case study to help answer these questions, with methods drawn from the literature on dynamical systems theory.
  We use the growing gecko NCA of Mordvintsev et al. (2020) with deterministic cell updates as a case study. To the best of the authors' knowledge, we present the first visualizations of NCA attractor dynamics. We also analyze them using the Lyapunov and Fourier spectra, to reveal that the NCA displays oscillatory, periodic and quasi-periodic behavior, and that these behaviors arise early during training. This challenges the belief that NCAs learn fixed point attractors. Finally, we show that large perturbations to the attractor states can throw the NCAs into a secondary mode separate from the original attractor. We hope that this initial foray into NCA attractor dynamics expands the toolkit for NCA researchers to analyze the robustness and stability of their systems.

</details>

### [55] [GRACE: A Dynamic Coreset Selection Framework for Large Language Model Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11810)
*Tianhao Tang,Haoyang Li,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出GRACE框架，通过图引导的自适应动态核心集选择来加速大语言模型训练，结合表示多样性和梯度重要性指标，显著提升训练效率和下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数庞大、架构复杂，训练成本高昂。现有核心集选择方法无法适应LLM训练的动态特性，且在大规模模型上可扩展性不足，需要更高效的训练优化方案。

Method: 提出GRACE框架：1) 动态构建和更新核心集，结合表示多样性和梯度重要性指标；2) 使用k-NN图传播机制降低频繁更新的计算成本；3) 选择性更新分数和嵌入，适应训练动态变化。

Result: 在三个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，GRACE显著提高了训练效率，并在不同LLM和任务上改善了下游性能。

Conclusion: GRACE框架通过图引导的自适应动态核心集选择，有效解决了LLM训练中的效率和可扩展性问题，为大规模语言模型的高效训练提供了新方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in natural language understanding and generation. However, their immense number of parameters and complex transformer-based architectures result in significant resource demands and computational complexity during training, making it challenging to optimize them efficiently on large datasets. To reduce training costs while preserving performance, researchers have investigated coreset selection techniques, which aim to identify small, representative subsets of the entire training dataset to accelerate LLM training. However, existing coreset selection methods fail to adapt to the dynamic nature of LLM training and often struggle with scalability for models of this size. To address these limitations, we propose a graph-guided adaptive and dynamic coreset selection framework for LLMs, namely GRACE. GRACE dynamically constructs and updates coresets by combining representation diversity with gradient-based importance metrics, ensuring both informativeness and efficiency. To mitigate the computational cost of frequent updates, GRACE leverages a $k$-NN graph-based propagation mechanism and selectively updates scores and embeddings, adapting to evolving training dynamics. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that GRACE significantly improves training efficiency and downstream performance across diverse LLMs and tasks.

</details>


### [56] [Lit2Vec: A Reproducible Workflow for Building a Legally Screened Chemistry Corpus from S2ORC for Downstream Retrieval and Text Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12498)
*Mahmoud Amiri,Jamile Mohammad Jafari,Sara Mostafapour,Thomas Bocklitz*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Lit2Vec：一个可复现的工作流程，用于从Semantic Scholar开放研究语料库中构建和验证化学领域语料库，包含58万篇化学全文研究文章，具有结构化文本、段落级嵌入和元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个高质量、可复现的化学领域语料库，支持下游的信息检索和文本挖掘应用，同时确保版权合规性和技术验证。

Method: 使用基于元数据的保守版权筛选方法，从Semantic Scholar开放研究语料库中提取化学相关文章；生成结构化全文、段落分块、使用intfloat/e5-large-v2模型生成段落级嵌入；利用Unpaywall、OpenAlex和Crossref进行版权验证；对语料库进行模式合规性、嵌入可复现性、文本质量和元数据完整性的技术验证。

Result: 构建了包含582,683篇化学特定全文研究文章的内部研究语料库，具有结构化全文、段落级嵌入和完整元数据；部分语料库还添加了机器生成的摘要和涵盖18个化学领域的多标签子领域标注。

Conclusion: Lit2Vec提供了一个可复现的语料库构建和验证工作流程，包括代码、重建工作流程、模式、元数据/来源工件和验证输出，使研究人员能够使用公开的上游数据集和元数据服务复现该语料库。

Abstract: We present Lit2Vec, a reproducible workflow for constructing and validating a chemistry corpus from the Semantic Scholar Open Research Corpus using conservative, metadata-based license screening. Using this workflow, we assembled an internal study corpus of 582,683 chemistry-specific full-text research articles with structured full text, token-aware paragraph chunks, paragraph-level embeddings generated with the intfloat/e5-large-v2 model, and record-level metadata including abstracts and licensing information. To support downstream retrieval and text-mining use cases, an eligible subset of the corpus was additionally enriched with machine-generated brief summaries and multi-label subfield annotations spanning 18 chemistry domains. Licensing was screened using metadata from Unpaywall, OpenAlex, and Crossref, and the resulting corpus was technically validated for schema compliance, embedding reproducibility, text quality, and metadata completeness. The primary contribution of this work is a reproducible workflow for corpus construction and validation, together with its associated schema and reproducibility resources. The released materials include the code, reconstruction workflow, schema, metadata/provenance artifacts, and validation outputs needed to reproduce the corpus from pinned public upstream resources. Public redistribution of source-derived text and broad text-derived representations is outside the scope of the general release. Researchers can reproduce the workflow by using the released pipeline with publicly available upstream datasets and metadata services.

</details>


### [57] [ROSE: An Intent-Centered Evaluation Metric for NL2SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12988)
*Wenqi Pei,Shizheng Hou,Boyan Li,Han Chen,Zhichao Shi,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文提出ROSE，一种意图中心化的NL2SQL评估指标，通过对抗性证明-反驳级联机制，专注于预测SQL是否回答了问题，而非与参考SQL的语法一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前广泛使用的执行准确率(EX)指标存在多个问题：对语法变化敏感、忽略问题可能有多种解释、容易被错误的ground-truth SQL误导，导致NL2SQL评估不可靠。

Method: 提出ROSE指标，采用对抗性证明-反驳级联机制：SQL证明器独立评估预测SQL相对于用户意图的语义正确性；对抗性反驳器使用ground-truth SQL作为证据来挑战和精炼这一判断。

Result: 在专家对齐的验证集ROSE-VEC上，ROSE与人类专家的Cohen's Kappa一致性最佳，比次优指标高出近24%。对19种NL2SQL方法的大规模重新评估揭示了四个有价值的见解。

Conclusion: ROSE解决了传统EX指标的局限性，提供了更可靠的NL2SQL评估方法。作者发布了ROSE和ROSE-VEC以促进更可靠的NL2SQL研究。

Abstract: Execution Accuracy (EX), the widely used metric for evaluating the effectiveness of Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) solutions, is becoming increasingly unreliable. It is sensitive to syntactic variation, ignores that questions may admit multiple interpretations, and is easily misled by erroneous ground-truth SQL. To address this, we introduce ROSE, an intent-centered metric that focuses on whether the predicted SQL answers the question, rather than consistency with the ground-truth SQL under the reference-dependent paradigm. ROSE employs an adversarial Prover-Refuter cascade: SQL Prover assesses the semantic correctness of a predicted SQL against the user's intent independently, while Adversarial Refuter uses the ground-truth SQL as evidence to challenge and refine this judgment. On our expert-aligned validation set ROSE-VEC, ROSE achieves the best agreement with human experts, outperforming the next-best metric by nearly 24% in Cohen's Kappa. We also conduct a largescale re-evaluation of 19 NL2SQL methods, revealing four valuable insights. We release ROSE and ROSE-VEC to facilitate more reliable NL2SQL research.

</details>

### [58] [M$^\star$: Every Task Deserves Its Own Memory Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11811)
*Wenbo Pan,Shujie Liu,Xiangyang Zhou,Shiwei Zhang,Wanlu Shi,Mirror Xu,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: M$^\star$：通过可执行程序演化自动发现任务优化的记忆系统，将记忆系统建模为Python程序，包含数据模式、存储逻辑和智能体工作流指令，通过反射式代码演化方法优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体记忆系统通常采用固定设计，针对特定领域（如对话的语义检索或编码的技能重用）优化，但为某一目的优化的记忆系统往往无法迁移到其他任务

Method: 将智能体记忆系统建模为Python编写的记忆程序，包含数据模式、存储逻辑和智能体工作流指令；使用反射式代码演化方法联合优化这些组件，采用基于种群的搜索策略，分析评估失败以迭代改进候选程序

Result: 在对话、具身规划和专家推理四个不同基准测试中，M$^\star$在所有评估任务上均稳健地优于现有固定记忆基线；演化的记忆程序在每个领域展现出结构上不同的处理机制

Conclusion: 为给定任务专门化记忆机制探索了广阔的设计空间，相比通用记忆范式提供了更优解决方案；自动发现任务优化的记忆系统是可行且有效的

Abstract: Large language model agents rely on specialized memory systems to accumulate and reuse knowledge during extended interactions. Recent architectures typically adopt a fixed memory design tailored to specific domains, such as semantic retrieval for conversations or skills reused for coding. However, a memory system optimized for one purpose frequently fails to transfer to others. To address this limitation, we introduce M$^\star$, a method that automatically discovers task-optimized memory harnesses through executable program evolution. Specifically, M$^\star$ models an agent memory system as a memory program written in Python. This program encapsulates the data Schema, the storage Logic, and the agent workflow Instructions. We optimize these components jointly using a reflective code evolution method; this approach employs a population-based search strategy and analyzes evaluation failures to iteratively refine the candidate programs. We evaluate M$^\star$ on four distinct benchmarks spanning conversation, embodied planning, and expert reasoning. Our results demonstrate that M$^\star$ improves performance over existing fixed-memory baselines robustly across all evaluated tasks. Furthermore, the evolved memory programs exhibit structurally distinct processing mechanisms for each domain. This finding indicates that specializing the memory mechanism for a given task explores a broad design space and provides a superior solution compared to general-purpose memory paradigms.

</details>


### [59] [Polyregular equivalence is undecidable in higher-order types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11935)
*Mikołaj Bojańczyk,Grzegorz Fabiański,Rafał Stefański*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文证明了在基于λ演算定义的高阶多正则函数扩展中，函数等价性是不可判定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字符串到字符串的多正则函数等价性是否可判定是一个开放问题。本文研究其基于λ演算的高阶扩展，旨在确定在这种扩展设置下等价性的可判定性。

Method: 采用基于Bojańczyk (2018) λ演算定义的多正则函数高阶扩展，通过从铺砖问题归约来证明等价性的不可判定性。

Result: 证明了在基于λ演算的高阶多正则函数扩展中，函数等价性是不可判定的。

Conclusion: 对于基于λ演算定义的高阶多正则函数，函数等价性判定问题是不可判定的，这通过从铺砖问题的归约得到证明。

Abstract: It is open whether equivalence ( f = g ) is decidable for string-to-string polyregular functions. We consider their higher-order extension based on the λ-calculus definition of polyregular functions from Bojańczyk (2018). In this setting, equivalence is undecidable by reduction from the tiling problem.

</details>


### [60] [Modular Verification of Differential Privacy in Probabilistic Higher-Order Separation Logic (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12713)
*Philipp G. Haselwarter,Alejandro Aguirre,Simon Oddershede Gregersen,Kwing Hei Li,Joseph Tassarotti,Lars Birkedal*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出一种适用于复杂通用编程语言的差分隐私程序验证逻辑，支持隐私预算作为分离逻辑资源，能够处理隐私过滤器、缓存等常见编程模式，并实现了一个经过验证的差分隐私库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私验证方法难以处理现代DP库的设计模式和语言特性，以及库设计者希望建立的保证类别。需要一种能够验证复杂通用编程语言中差分隐私实现的方法。

Method: 引入一种程序逻辑，将隐私预算作为分离逻辑资源进行推理。该逻辑具有表达能力强的目标语言，能够处理隐私过滤器、缓存等常见编程模式，支持对原语、高阶组合器和交互式算法的模块化推理。

Result: 实现了一个经过验证的差分隐私机制库，包括稀疏向量技术的在线版本，以及受OpenDP启发的隐私过滤器。展示了规范的可重用性，通过实例化验证了库的客户端。所有结果都在Rocq Prover中进行了基础验证。

Conclusion: 提出的程序逻辑能够有效验证复杂通用编程语言中的差分隐私实现，解决了现有验证方法的局限性，为DP库的可靠性提供了形式化保证。

Abstract: Differential privacy is the standard method for privacy-preserving data analysis. The importance of having strong guarantees on the reliability of implementations of differentially private algorithms is widely recognized and has sparked fruitful research on formal methods. However, the design patterns and language features used in modern DP libraries as well as the classes of guarantees that the library designers wish to establish often fall outside of the scope of previous verification approaches.
  We introduce a program logic suitable for verifying differentially private implementations written in complex, general-purpose programming languages. Our logic has first-class support for reasoning about privacy budgets as a separation logic resource. The expressiveness of the logic and the target language allow our approach to handle common programming patterns used in the implementation of libraries for differential privacy, such as privacy filters and caching. While previous work has focused on developing guarantees for programs written in domain-specific languages or for privacy mechanisms in isolation, our logic can reason modularly about primitives, higher-order combinators, and interactive algorithms.
  We demonstrate the applicability of our approach by implementing a verified library of differential privacy mechanisms, including an online version of the Sparse Vector Technique, as well as a privacy filter inspired by the popular Python library OpenDP, which crucially relies on our ability to handle the combination of randomization, local state, and higher-order functions. We demonstrate that our specifications are general and reusable by instantiating them to verify clients of our library. All of our results have been foundationally verified in the Rocq Prover.

</details>


### [61] [Hyper Separation Logic (extended version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12870)
*Trayan Gospodinov,Thibault Dardinier,Peter Müller*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: HSL是首个支持堆操作程序上任意量词交替的超性质模块化推理的程序逻辑，扩展了Hyper Hoare Logic并引入了超分离合取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分离逻辑只能处理特定类别的超性质（如∀∀-超性质或∃∃-性质），不支持量词交替（如广义非干扰GNI）。Hyper Hoare Logic虽然能处理这类性质，但不支持堆操作程序，无法应用于常见命令式程序。

Method: 提出超分离逻辑（HSL），将Hyper Hoare Logic扩展到堆操作程序，引入新颖的超分离合取将标准分离合取提升到状态集合，实现超性质的广义框架规则。

Result: 在Isabelle/HOL中证明了HSL的可靠性，展示了其对现有分离逻辑无法处理的超性质的表达能力。

Conclusion: HSL是首个支持堆操作程序上任意量词交替超性质模块化推理的程序逻辑，填补了现有技术的空白。

Abstract: Many important functional and security properties--including non-interference, determinism, and generalized non-interference (GNI)--are hyperproperties, i.e., properties relating multiple executions of a program. Existing separation logics allow one to reason about specific classes of hyperproperties, e.g., $\forall\forall$-hyperproperties such as non-interference and $\exists\exists$-properties such as non-determinism. However, they do not support quantifier alternation, which is for instance needed to express GNI. The only existing logic that can reason about such properties is Hyper Hoare Logic, but it does not support heap-manipulating programs and, thus, is not applicable to common imperative programs.
  This paper introduces Hyper Separation Logic (HSL), the first program logic that supports modular reasoning about hyperproperties with arbitrary quantifier alternation over programs that manipulate the heap. HSL generalizes Hyper Hoare Logic with a novel hyper separating conjunction that lifts the standard separating conjunction to sets of states, enabling a generalized frame rule for hyperproperties. We prove HSL sound in Isabelle/HOL and demonstrate its expressiveness for hyperproperties that lie beyond the reach of existing separation logics.

</details>


### [62] [Towards a Linear-Algebraic Hypervisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12902)
*Breandan Considine*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出了一种并行虚拟机，用于并行评估数百万个并发数组程序，相比串行评估实现了高达147倍的加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序合成、超级优化和数组编程中的许多技术需要并行执行通用程序。GPU虽然能够进行领域特定的并行计算，但在这些工作负载中传统上未被充分利用

Method: 引入了一个令人愉悦的并行虚拟机，通过评估数百万个并发数组程序来基准测试其性能

Result: 观察到相对于串行评估，速度提升高达147倍

Conclusion: 该并行虚拟机能够有效利用GPU的并行能力，显著加速程序合成、超级优化和数组编程中的并行工作负载

Abstract: Many techniques in program synthesis, superoptimization, and array programming require parallel rollouts of general-purpose programs. GPUs, while capable targets for domain-specific parallelism, are traditionally underutilized by such workloads. Motivated by this opportunity, we introduce a pleasingly parallel virtual machine and benchmark its performance by evaluating millions of concurrent array programs, observing speedups up to $147\times$ relative to serial evaluation.

</details>

### [63] [Evaluating Lightweight Block Cipher Payload Encryption for Real-Time CAN Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11853)
*Kevin Setterstrom,Jeremy Straub*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 在QT PY ESP32-S2微控制器上实现轻量级分组密码对CAN总线负载加密，评估其防止基于语义分类的逆向工程的能力，同时保持100Hz传输频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAN总线未加密的负载容易被攻击者通过观察和统计分析进行语义分类逆向工程，推断信号含义。需要研究在资源受限的嵌入式硬件上实现加密的可行性。

Method: 使用QT PY ESP32-S2微控制器实现轻量级分组密码对CAN负载进行加密，通过实验测量时间影响、负载模式可观察性和基于相关性的推断能力。

Result: 加密成功掩盖了常数值和可预测的信号模式，同时保持了100Hz的传输调度。轻量级负载加密能减少被动观察式CAN信号语义推断，对时序开销影响有限。

Conclusion: 轻量级负载加密可在资源受限硬件上有效防止基于语义分类的CAN总线逆向工程，且对实时性能影响可控。

Abstract: This study evaluates the feasibility of integrating lightweight block cipher payload encryption into a real-time embedded controller area network (CAN) node using a QT PY ESP32-S2 microcontroller. This work seeks to determine whether the use of a block cipher can prevent semantic taxonomy-based reverse engineering, which infers signal meaning from unencrypted CAN traffic using observation and statistical analysis. CAN payloads are encrypted using a lightweight block cipher and evaluated through experiments that measure timing impact, payload pattern observability, and correlation-based inference. Results indicate that encryption masks constant values and predictable signal patterns while preserving a 100 Hz transmission schedule. These findings suggest that lightweight payload encryption can reduce passive, observation based inference of CAN signal semantics on resource-constrained hardware with limited timing overhead impact.

</details>


### [64] [COBALT-TLA: A Neuro-Symbolic Verification Loop for Cross-Chain Bridge Vulnerability Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12172)
*Dominik Blain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: COBALT-TLA是一个神经符号验证循环，将LLM与TLA+模型检查器TLC结合，自动生成和验证跨链桥协议规范，能快速发现漏洞包括未提示的攻击类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM在形式化方法中的幻觉问题，通过确定性证明反馈将零样本代码生成转化为收敛的证明发现策略，提高跨链桥协议验证的自动化程度和准确性。

Method: 构建神经符号验证循环：LLM生成有界TLA+规范，TLC作为语义预言机进行模型检查，解析结构化错误轨迹并注入模型上下文驱动收敛，形成自动化REPL环境。

Result: 在三个跨链桥目标（包括Nomad 1.9亿美元漏洞的忠实模型）上评估，最多2次迭代即达到BUG_FOUND状态，TLC执行时间始终低于0.30秒，自主发现了未提示的Optimistic Relay Attack漏洞类型。

Conclusion: 确定性证明反馈足以消除LLM在形式化方法中的幻觉，将零样本代码生成转化为收敛的证明发现策略，COBALT-TLA展示了神经符号验证在协议安全分析中的有效性。

Abstract: We present COBALT-TLA, a neuro-symbolic verification loop that pairs an LLM with TLC, the TLA+ model checker, in an automated REPL. The LLM generates bounded TLA+ specifications; TLC acts as a semantic oracle; structured error traces are parsed and injected back into the model's context to drive convergence. We evaluate the system against three cross-chain bridge targets, including a faithful model of the Nomad $190M exploit. COBALT-TLA reaches a verified BUG_FOUND state in at most 2 iterations on all targets, with TLC execution consistently below 0.30 seconds. Notably, the system autonomously discovers an unprompted vulnerability class -- the Optimistic Relay Attack -- not present in the human-written baseline specification. We argue that deterministic prover feedback is sufficient to neutralize LLM hallucination in formal methods, transforming zero-shot code generation into a convergent proof-finding strategy.

</details>


### [65] [Can we Watermark Low-Entropy LLM Outputs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12051)
*Noam Mazor,Andrew Morgan,Rafael Pass*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在大型语言模型输出中实现可证明不可检测水印的方案，特别关注在每令牌熵仅为常数（而非与字母表大小或安全参数相关）的低熵条件下的水印构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可证明不可检测水印方案需要输出具有常数熵率（即输出中足够长子串的令牌需要有O(log|T|)的经验熵），且Golowich等人的方案还要求字母表T大于安全参数。本文旨在探索在每令牌熵仅为常数的低熵条件下是否也能实现水印。

Method: 在每令牌熵仅为常数的条件下，构建了两种水印方案：1）基于亚指数LPN假设（与Christ等人相同）的抗随机替换水印方案；2）抗随机替换和随机删除的水印方案，需要额外假设LLM输出仅引入随机错误，或构建抗对抗替换和随机删除的伪随机纠错码。

Result: 成功在低熵条件下（每令牌熵仅为常数）构建了水印方案，突破了现有方案对熵率的要求限制，扩展了可证明不可检测水印的应用范围。

Conclusion: 本文证明了即使在每令牌熵仅为常数的低熵条件下，也能构造可证明不可检测的水印方案，为LLM输出水印提供了更广泛的理论基础，特别是在输出熵较低的场景下。

Abstract: A recent and exciting thread of work focuses on developing methods for watermarking the output of large language models (LLMs). We focus on provably undetectable watermarking-that is, schemes that do not alter the output distribution of the LLM, yet enable embedding a watermark in the output that identifies the output as having been generated by the particular LLM. Furthermore, the watermark should be hard to remove by an adversary that may potentially edit, insert, or delete tokens from the watermarked output. Indeed, recent work (Christ et al. [COLT'24], Christ et al. [CRYPTO'24], Golowich et al. [NeuroIPS'24]) shows how to develop such schemes that are robust against a constant fraction of substitutions, or even against a constant fraction of arbitrary edits.
  These works, however, make strong assumptions on the entropy present in the output of the LLM. Most notably, they all require constant entropy rate-that is, a constant fraction of the tokens in a sufficiently long substring of the output need to have empirical entropy at least O(log |T|), where T is the alphabet of tokens, and Golowich et al. additionally require T to be larger than the security parameter. In this work, we consider whether we can also watermark the outputs of LLMs when the per-token entropy is just a constant, discarding the dependence on the alphabet size or security parameter. In this regime, we construct:
  - A watermarking scheme robust against random substitutions (assuming subexponential LPN, as in Christ et al. [CRYPTO'24])
  - A watermarking scheme robust against random substitutions and random deletions, given either the additional heuristic assumption that the output of the LLM only introduces random errors (analogous to the assumption made by Christ et al. [CRYPTO'24]) or a construction of a pseudorandom error-correcting code robust to adversarial substitutions and random deletions.

</details>


### [66] [VeriX-Anon: A Multi-Layered Framework for Mathematically Verifiable Outsourced Target-Driven Data Anonymization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12431)
*Miit Daga,Swarna Priya Ramu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VeriX-Anon是一个用于外包目标驱动k-匿名化的多层验证框架，结合确定性哈希、概率边界哨兵和XAI指纹三种机制，在12个对抗场景中成功检测11个异常，客户端验证可在百万行数据下1秒内完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织越来越多地将隐私敏感数据转换外包给云提供商，但目前缺乏实用机制让数据所有者验证外包算法是否被忠实执行，需要解决外包隐私保护算法执行的可验证性问题。

Method: 采用三层正交验证机制：1) 确定性验证：通过认证决策树的Merkle风格哈希；2) 概率验证：在随机森林决策边界附近部署边界哨兵和具有加密标识符的精确重复双胞胎；3) 效用验证：通过可解释AI指纹技术，使用Wasserstein距离比较匿名化前后的SHAP值分布。

Result: 在三个跨领域数据集上对抗三种攻击者（Lazy、Dumb、Approximate），VeriX-Anon在12个场景中正确检测到11个异常。XAI层是唯一能检测Approximate攻击者的机制，在Adult和Bank数据集上成功，但在严重不平衡的Diabetes数据集上失败。目标驱动匿名化比盲目匿名化保留更多效用（p=0.000977，Cohen's d=1.96，平均F1差距+0.1574）。客户端验证在百万行数据下1秒内完成。

Conclusion: VeriX-Anon通过多层验证框架有效解决了外包隐私保护算法的可验证性问题，三层机制互补增强了检测能力。目标驱动匿名化相比盲目方法显著提升效用保留。XAI层对平衡数据集有效但对严重不平衡数据集有限制，需要自适应阈值调整。哨兵规避概率在平衡数据集接近零，不平衡数据集达0.52，但双胞胎层在所有测试场景中提供了补偿。

Abstract: Organisations increasingly outsource privacy-sensitive data transformations to cloud providers, yet no practical mechanism lets the data owner verify that the contracted algorithm was faithfully executed. VeriX-Anon is a multi-layered verification framework for outsourced Target-Driven k-anonymization combining three orthogonal mechanisms: deterministic verification via Merkle-style hashing of an Authenticated Decision Tree, probabilistic verification via Boundary Sentinels near the Random Forest decision boundary and exact-duplicate Twins with cryptographic identifiers, and utility-based verification via Explainable AI fingerprinting that compares SHAP value distributions before and after anonymization using the Wasserstein distance. Evaluated on three cross-domain datasets against Lazy (drops 5 percent of records), Dumb (random splitting, fake hash), and Approximate (random splitting, valid hash) adversaries, VeriX-Anon correctly detected deviations in 11 of 12 scenarios. No single layer achieved this alone. The XAI layer was the only mechanism that caught the Approximate adversary, succeeding on Adult and Bank but failing on the severely imbalanced Diabetes dataset where class imbalance suppresses the SHAP signal, confirming the need for adaptive thresholding. An 11-point k-sweep showed Target-Driven anonymization preserves significantly more utility than Blind anonymization (Wilcoxon $p = 0.000977$, Cohen's $d = 1.96$, mean F1 gap $+0.1574$). Client-side verification completes under one second at one million rows. The threat model covers three empirically evaluated profiles and one theoretical profile (Informed Attacker) aware of trap embedding but unable to defeat the cryptographic salt. Sentinel evasion probability ranges from near-zero for balanced datasets to 0.52 for imbalanced ones, a limitation the twin layer compensates for in every tested scenario.

</details>


### [67] [Fully Homomorphic Encryption on Llama 3 model for privacy preserving LLM inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12168)
*Anes Abdennebi,Nadjia Kara,Laaziz Lahlou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将后量子密码学（PQC）基于格的同态加密（HE）集成到LLM推理管道中的方法，以保护LLAMA-3模型的数据隐私，抵御量子计算攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能（GenAI）在医疗、金融、交通和信息安全等数据驱动领域的应用带来了效率提升，但也引发了大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全问题。不安全的LLM管道可能导致数据泄露、秘密泄露，使系统易受数据投毒、提示注入和模型窃取等攻击。现有安全技术（输入/输出净化、去中心化学习、访问控制管理和加密）仍面临量子计算攻击的威胁，量子计算可能破解现有加密算法。

Method: 修改LLAMA-3模型的Transformer架构推理管道，注入基于concrete-ml库提供的同态加密主要操作。将后量子密码学（PQC）基于格的同态加密（HE）功能集成到LLM推理管道中，保护其部分层免受数据隐私攻击。

Result: 在i9 CPU上实现了高达98%的文本生成准确率，延迟为237毫秒，达到每秒80个令牌的生成速度。这证明了运行FHE（全同态加密）保护的LLAMA-3推理模型的可行性和有效性。进一步实验和分析验证了模型的文本生成延迟和行为。

Conclusion: 该研究成功展示了将后量子同态加密集成到LLM推理管道中的可行性，为抵御量子计算攻击提供了有效的隐私保护方案，在保持高文本生成准确率的同时实现了合理的延迟性能。

Abstract: The applications of Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) and their intersections with data-driven fields, such as healthcare, finance, transportation, and information security, have led to significant improvements in service efficiency and low latency. However, this synergy raises serious concerns regarding the security of large language models (LLMs) and their potential impact on the privacy of companies and users' data. Many technology companies that incorporate LLMs in their services with a certain level of command and control bear a risk of data exposure and secret divulgence caused by insecure LLM pipelines, making them vulnerable to multiple attacks such as data poisoning, prompt injection, and model theft. Although several security techniques (input/output sanitization, decentralized learning, access control management, and encryption) were implemented to reduce this risk, there is still an imminent risk of quantum computing attacks, which are expected to break existing encryption algorithms, hence, retrieving secret keys, encrypted sensitive data, and decrypting encrypted models. In this extensive work, we integrate the Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) based Lattice-based Homomorphic Encryption (HE) main functions in the LLM's inference pipeline to secure some of its layers against data privacy attacks. We modify the inference pipeline of the transformer architecture for the LLAMA-3 model while injecting the main homomorphic encryption operations provided by the concrete-ml library. We demonstrate high text generation accuracies (up to 98%) with reasonable latencies (237 ms) on an i9 CPU, reaching up to 80 tokens per second, which proves the feasibility and validity of our work while running a FHE-secured LLAMA-3 inference model. Further experiments and analysis are discussed to justify models' text generation latencies and behaviours.

</details>


### [68] [Mitigating S-RAHA: An On-device Framework to Prevent Forwarding of Re-Captured Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12178)
*Keshav Sood,Iynkaran Natgunanathan,Purathani Praitheeshan,Praitheeshan Kirupananthan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SR-AHA攻击概念（屏幕重拍模拟漏洞攻击），开发基于深度学习的设备端重拍检测框架，结合自动阻止分享机制和隐形元数据标识符概念


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有截图防护机制仅针对数字截图，无法防止物理层面的屏幕重拍攻击（SR-AHA），导致敏感图像可被绕过安全措施自由转发，造成隐私、人身安全和取证风险

Method: 开发设备端框架：1）深度学习辅助的重拍检测模型，可在多样环境条件下区分原始数字内容和屏幕重拍图像；2）设备端执行机制自动阻止可疑重拍图像在应用间分享；3）引入隐形元数据标识符（IMI）概念，嵌入受保护图像以实现泄漏路径的取证追踪

Result: 提出完整的SR-AHA攻击概念和防护框架，包括可工作的重拍检测模型和自动阻止机制，以及概念性的IMI组件，为客户端安全架构提供轻量级、隐形标识符的集成方向

Conclusion: SR-AHA攻击暴露了现有数字防护方案的物理层面漏洞，提出的设备端框架通过深度学习检测、自动阻止机制和隐形标识符概念，为移动应用提供了针对物理屏幕重拍攻击的综合防护方案

Abstract: Protecting sensitive visual content from unauthorized redistribution is a growing challenge for privacy focused mobile applications, including dating platforms. Screenshot prevention mechanisms, rely on server side monitoring or are limited to digital screenshot detection, are commonly deployed to stop forwarding sensitive images. However, an adversary uses another smartphone to take a photo of the mobile screen, in this scenario the existing solutions offer no protection against psychically screen recapture attacks. Since the attack happens in the physical plane rather than on a digital plane and shows a void or hole in the existing solutions, we name this the Screen Recaptured Analog Hole Attack (S RAHA). Such physically recaptured images bypass digital safeguards and can be freely forwarded, creating substantial privacy, personal safety, and forensic risks. We present a low computational secure by design on device framework that aims to detect and prevent the forwarding of recaptured images directly to the users device. The proposed system integrates a deep learning assisted recapture detection model capable of distinguishing original digital content from camera to screen captures under diverse environmental conditions, together with an on device enforcement mechanism that automatically blocks the sharing of suspected recaptured images between applications. We also introduce the concept of an invisible metadata identifier (IMI) that can be embedded into protected images to enable forensic traceability of potential leakage paths. Although the IMI component is explored at a conceptual and feasibility level rather than fully implemented, it demonstrates a promising direction for integrating lightweight, invisible identifiers into client side security architectures.

</details>


### [69] [TimeMark: A Trustworthy Time Watermarking Framework for Exact Generation-Time Recovery from AIGC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12216)
*Shangkun Che,Silin Du,Ge Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出可信水印概念，通过密码学技术和监管监督实现100%准确的时间水印恢复，解决LLM生成文本的知识产权争议证据问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方法依赖token分布的统计信号，导致概率性检测和可靠性降低，易受伪造攻击，且模型提供商可任意伪造水印，无法满足司法证据要求

Method: 提出可信水印框架，集成密码学技术，在监管监督下将时间信息编码到时间相关的密钥中；水印载荷与时间解耦，为每个实例生成随机、非存储的比特序列；设计两阶段编码机制结合纠错码，实现理论完美准确的时间恢复

Result: 理论分析和实验证明该框架满足司法证据的可靠性要求，能抵抗用户端统计攻击和提供商端伪造攻击，为未来AIGC相关知识产权争议提供实用解决方案

Conclusion: 可信水印概念通过密码学技术和监管架构解决了现有水印方法的根本缺陷，实现了可靠的时间水印恢复，为LLM生成文本的知识产权保护提供了可行的司法证据机制

Abstract: The widespread use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in text generation has raised increasing concerns about intellectual property disputes. Watermarking techniques, which embed meta information into AI-generated content (AIGC), have the potential to serve as judicial evidence. However, existing methods rely on statistical signals in token distributions, leading to inherently probabilistic detection and reduced reliability, especially in multi-bit encoding (e.g., timestamps). Moreover, such methods introduce detectable statistical patterns, making them vulnerable to forgery attacks and enabling model providers to fabricate arbitrary watermarks. To address these issues, we propose the concept of trustworthy watermark, which achieves reliable recovery with 100% identification accuracy while resisting both user-side statistical attacks and provider-side forgery. We focus on trustworthy time watermarking for use as judicial evidence. Our framework integrates cryptographic techniques and encodes time information into time-dependent secret keys under regulatory supervision, preventing arbitrary timestamp fabrication. The watermark payload is decoupled from time and generated as a random, non-stored bit sequence for each instance, eliminating statistical patterns. To ensure verifiability, we design a two-stage encoding mechanism, which, combined with error-correcting codes, enables reliable recovery of generation time with theoretically perfect accuracy. Both theoretical analysis and experiments demonstrate that our framework satisfies the reliability requirements for judicial evidence and offers a practical solution for future AIGC-related intellectual property disputes.

</details>


### [70] [TEMPLATEFUZZ: Fine-Grained Chat Template Fuzzing for Jailbreaking and Red Teaming LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12232)
*Qingchao Shen,Zibo Xiao,Lili Huang,Enwei Hu,Yongqiang Tian,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TEMPLATEFUZZ：针对LLM聊天模板的细粒度模糊测试框架，通过元素级变异规则和启发式搜索策略，显著提升越狱攻击成功率，同时保持模型准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在广泛部署中存在越狱攻击漏洞，现有研究主要关注提示注入攻击，需要大量提示工程且忽略了聊天模板这一关键攻击面。聊天模板作为LLM输入处理的关键组件，其安全性尚未得到充分探索

Method: TEMPLATEFUZZ框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 元素级变异规则生成多样化的聊天模板变体；(2) 启发式搜索策略引导模板生成方向，在提升攻击成功率的同时保持模型准确性；(3) 基于主动学习的轻量级规则化oracle，用于准确高效的越狱评估

Result: 在12个开源LLM上评估，平均攻击成功率98.2%，准确性仅下降1.1%，优于现有方法9.1%-47.9%的攻击成功率和8.4%的准确性下降。在5个商业LLM上，通过聊天模板提示注入攻击达到90%的平均攻击成功率

Conclusion: TEMPLATEFUZZ证明了聊天模板是LLM安全的关键攻击面，提出的细粒度模糊测试框架能有效发现和利用模板漏洞，为LLM安全评估提供了新视角和实用工具

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across diverse domains, yet their vulnerability to jailbreak attacks, where adversarial inputs bypass safety mechanisms to elicit harmful outputs, poses significant security risks. While prior work has primarily focused on prompt injection attacks, these approaches often require resource-intensive prompt engineering and overlook other critical components, such as chat templates. This paper introduces TEMPLATEFUZZ, a fine-grained fuzzing framework that systematically exposes vulnerabilities in chat templates, a critical yet underexplored attack surface in LLMs. Specifically, TEMPLATEFUZZ (1) designs a series of element-level mutation rules to generate diverse chat template variants, (2) proposes a heuristic search strategy to guide the chat template generation toward the direction of amplifying the attack success rate (ASR) while preserving model accuracy, and (3) integrates an active learning-based strategy to derive a lightweight rule-based oracle for accurate and efficient jailbreak evaluation. Evaluated on twelve open-source LLMs across multiple attack scenarios, TEMPLATEFUZZ achieves an average ASR of 98.2% with only 1.1% accuracy degradation, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 9.1%-47.9% in ASR and 8.4% in accuracy degradation. Moreover, even on five industry-leading commercial LLMs where chat templates cannot be specified, TEMPLATEFUZZ attains a 90% average ASR via chat template-based prompt injection attacks.

</details>


### [71] [SpanKey: Dynamic Key Space Conditioning for Neural Network Access Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12254)
*WenBin Yan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SpanKey是一种轻量级的推理门控方法，通过将密钥注入激活值来实现访问控制，无需加密权重或追求排行榜精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种轻量级的推理门控机制，在不加密模型权重的情况下控制模型访问，避免传统加密方法的计算开销

Method: 使用基础矩阵B定义低维密钥子空间Span(B)，训练时采样系数α生成密钥k=α⊤B，通过加法或乘法映射以强度γ将密钥注入中间激活值

Result: 提出了密钥吸收失效模式的分析框架（Beta能量分割和边界尾部诊断），在CIFAR-10 ResNet-18和MNIST上进行了实验验证，展示了三种操作模式

Conclusion: SpanKey为轻量级推理门控提供了可行的机制，但明确不承诺密码学安全性，需要进一步研究扩展性和实际部署

Abstract: SpanKey is a lightweight way to gate inference without encrypting weights or chasing leaderboard accuracy on gated inference. The idea is to condition activations on secret keys. A basis matrix $B$ defines a low-dimensional key subspace $Span(B)$; during training we sample coefficients $α$ and form keys $k=α^\top B$, then inject them into intermediate activations with additive or multiplicative maps and strength $γ$. Valid keys lie in $Span(B)$; invalid keys are sampled outside that subspace. We make three points. (i) Mechanism: subspace key injection and a multi-layer design space. (ii) Failure mode: key absorption, together with two analytical results (a Beta-energy split and margin-tail diagnostics), explains weak baseline separation in energy and margin terms -- these are not a security theorem. iii) Deny losses and experiments: Modes A--C and extensions, with CIFAR-10 ResNet-18 runs and MNIST ablations for Mode B. We summarize setup and first-order analysis, injectors, absorption, deny losses and ablations, a threat discussion that does not promise cryptography, and closing remarks on scale. Code: \texttt{https://github.com/mindmemory-ai/dksc}

</details>


### [72] [UniDetect: LLM-Driven Universal Fraud Detection across Heterogeneous Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12329)
*Shuyi Miao,Wangjie Qiu,Shengda Zhuo,Fei Shen,Dan Lin,Xingtong Yu,Chua Tat-Seng,Zhiming Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: UniDetect是一种基于大语言模型的多链加密货币欺诈账户检测方法，利用领域知识生成通用交易摘要文本作为检测证据，通过两阶段交替训练增强多模态联合推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着跨链互操作性发展，DeFi协议使非法资金能够重组为统一的流动资产并在加密货币市场流动，这些操作可以绕过针对单个区块链的监控，从而削弱现有监管框架。

Method: 使用领域知识指导LLM生成适用于异构区块链账户的通用交易摘要文本作为欺诈账户检测证据；引入两阶段交替训练策略，基于文本证据和交易图模式持续动态增强多模态联合推理能力。

Result: 在多条区块链上的实验显示，UniDetect在Kolmogorov-Smirnov（KS）指标上比现有方法提升5.57%到7.58%；在跨链零样本检测中识别超过94.58%的欺诈账户；在非区块链数据上也表现良好，F1分数比现有方法提高6.06%。

Conclusion: UniDetect通过结合大语言模型生成的文本证据和交易图模式的多模态联合推理，有效解决了跨链欺诈账户检测问题，在多个区块链和跨链场景中表现出优越性能，并能推广到非区块链数据。

Abstract: As cross-chain interoperability advances, decentralized finance (DeFi) protocols enable illicit funds to be reorganized into uniform liquid assets that flow throughout the cryptocurrency market. Such operations can bypass monitoring targeted at individual blockchains and thereby weaken current regulatory frameworks. Motivated by these, we introduce UniDetect, a multi-chain cryptocurrency fraud account detection method based on large language models (LLMs). Specifically, we use domain knowledge to guide the LLM to generate general transaction summary texts applicable to heterogeneous blockchain accounts, which serve as evidence for fraud account detection. Furthermore, we introduce a two-stage alternating training strategy to continuously and dynamically enhance the multimodal joint reasoning for detecting fraudulent accounts based on both the textual evidence and the transaction graph patterns. Experiments on multiple blockchains show that UniDetect outperforms existing methods 5.57% to 7.58% in Kolmogorov-Smirnov (KS). For cross-chain zero-shot detection, UniDetect identifies over 94.58% of fraudulent accounts. It also generalizes well to non-blockchain data, delivering a 6.06% improvement in F1 over existing methods. The dataset and source code are available at https://github.com/msy0513/UniDetect.

</details>


### [73] [CoLA: A Choice Leakage Attack Framework to Expose Privacy Risks in Subset Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12342)
*Qi Li,Cheng-Long Wang,Yinzhi Cao,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文挑战了"训练数据子集选择能降低隐私风险"的直觉假设，提出子集训练会引入新的隐私泄露面，并设计了CoLA框架来系统分析这种隐私泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习通常选择部分数据进行训练（如视觉核心集选择、语言模型大规模过滤），这被认为是提高可扩展性的标准预处理方法。普遍直觉认为训练数据越少，隐私风险越低。本文挑战这一假设，指出子集训练并非隐私无风险：数据包含或排除的选择本身会引入新的隐私泄露面，泄露更多敏感信息。

Method: 提出CoLA（选择泄露攻击）统一框架分析子集选择中的隐私泄露。根据攻击者对侧信道信息的了解程度，定义两种攻击场景：子集感知侧信道攻击和黑盒攻击。在这两种场景下，研究子集训练特有的两种隐私面：1）训练成员MIA（TM-MIA），关注训练数据成员隐私；2）选择参与MIA（SP-MIA），关注参与子集选择过程的所有样本隐私。

Result: 在视觉和语言模型上的实验表明，现有威胁模型低估了子集训练的隐私风险：扩展的隐私面会泄露训练和选择成员信息，将风险从单个模型扩展到更广泛的ML生态系统。

Conclusion: 子集训练并非隐私无风险，数据选择过程本身会引入新的隐私泄露面。SP-MIA将成员隐私概念从模型训练扩展到整个数据-模型供应链，现有隐私评估框架需要重新考虑子集选择带来的隐私影响。

Abstract: Training models on a carefully chosen portion of data rather than the full dataset is now a standard preprocess for modern ML. From vision coreset selection to large-scale filtering in language models, it enables scalability with minimal utility loss. A common intuition is that training on fewer samples should also reduce privacy risks. In this paper, we challenge this assumption. We show that subset training is not privacy free: the very choices of which data are included or excluded can introduce new privacy surface and leak more sensitive information. Such information can be captured by adversaries either through side-channel metadata from the subset selection process or via the outputs of the target model. To systematically study this phenomenon, we propose CoLA (Choice Leakage Attack), a unified framework for analyzing privacy leakage in subset selection. In CoLA, depending on the adversary's knowledge of the side-channel information, we define two practical attack scenarios: Subset-aware Side-channel Attacks and Black-box Attacks. Under both scenarios, we investigate two privacy surfaces unique to subset training: (1) Training-membership MIA (TM-MIA), which concerns only the privacy of training data membership, and (2) Selection-participation MIA (SP-MIA), which concerns the privacy of all samples that participated in the subset selection process. Notably, SP-MIA enlarges the notion of membership from model training to the entire data-model supply chain. Experiments on vision and language models show that existing threat models underestimate subset-training privacy risks: the expanded privacy surface leaks both training and selection membership, extending risks from individual models to the broader ML ecosystem.

</details>


### [74] [Compiling Activation Steering into Weights via Null-Space Constraints for Stealthy Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12359)
*Rui Yin,Tianxu Han,Naen Xu,Changjiang Li,Ping He,Chunyi Zhou,Jun Wang,Zhihui Fu,Tianyu Du,Jinbao Li,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于内部表征的后门攻击方法，通过提取顺从与拒绝行为之间的转向向量，将其编译为仅在触发词出现时激活的权重修改，解决了现有方法只能强制肯定前缀但无法保证持续有害输出的可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐的大型语言模型部署扩大了供应链攻击面，攻击者可分发后门检查点。现有后门注入方法通常优化token级映射，强制肯定前缀（如"Sure"），但这不能保证持续有害输出——模型可能开始看似同意，但在几个解码步骤后恢复为安全对齐的拒绝。需要解决这一可靠性差距。

Method: 将后门目标从表面token转移到内部表征。提取捕获顺从与拒绝行为差异的转向向量，将其编译为仅在触发词存在时激活的持久权重修改。为保持隐蔽性和良性效用，施加零空间约束，使注入的编辑在干净输入上保持休眠状态。该方法高效，仅需少量示例且允许闭式解。

Result: 在多个安全对齐的LLM和越狱基准测试中，该方法实现了高触发攻击成功率，同时保持了非触发时的安全性和通用效用。

Conclusion: 通过将后门目标从表面token转移到内部表征，提出的方法解决了现有后门攻击的可靠性问题，实现了更持久和有效的攻击，同时保持了隐蔽性和模型效用。

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world pipelines, yet this deployment also enlarges the supply-chain attack surface: adversaries can distribute backdoored checkpoints that behave normally under standard evaluation but jailbreak when a hidden trigger is present. Recent post-hoc weight-editing methods offer an efficient approach to injecting such backdoors by directly modifying model weights to map a trigger to an attacker-specified response. However, existing methods typically optimize a token-level mapping that forces an affirmative prefix (e.g., ``Sure''), which does not guarantee sustained harmful output -- the model may begin with apparent agreement yet revert to safety-aligned refusal within a few decoding steps. We address this reliability gap by shifting the backdoor objective from surface tokens to internal representations. We extract a steering vector that captures the difference between compliant and refusal behaviors, and compile it into a persistent weight modification that activates only when the trigger is present. To preserve stealthiness and benign utility, we impose a null-space constraint so that the injected edit remains dormant on clean inputs. The method is efficient, requiring only a small set of examples and admitting a closed-form solution. Across multiple safety-aligned LLMs and jailbreak benchmarks, our method achieves high triggered attack success while maintaining non-triggered safety and general utility.

</details>


### [75] [Practical Evaluation of the Crypto-Agility Maturity Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12428)
*Leonie Wolf,Samson Umezulike,Gurur Öndarö,Sebastian Schinzel,Fabian Ising*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对CAMM首次评估发现其仅部分满足成熟度模型设计原则，存在范围模糊、标准操作性不足、依赖关系问题等缺陷，并提出改进建议


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码敏捷性是数字通信长期安全的关键前提，特别是在后量子密码学转型背景下。为系统评估这一能力，Hohm等人提出了密码敏捷性成熟度模型(CAMM)。本研究旨在首次评估CAMM是否符合成熟度模型的既定设计原则

Method: 采用成熟度模型的设计原则框架对CAMM进行系统性评估，分析其范围、目标群体、接受标准、依赖关系等关键要素。同时将CAMM应用于简单现实场景进行验证

Result: 分析显示CAMM仅部分满足设计原则：范围和目标群体模糊；接受标准操作性不足，限制了可验证性和可重复性；依赖关系存在冗余、循环和遗漏。现实场景应用进一步证实了这些问题，较高成熟度级别的多个要求不适用或不明确

Conclusion: 基于发现的问题，提出了对CAMM的具体改进建议，以实现更一致和可靠的密码敏捷性评估。这些改进将增强CAMM作为评估工具的有效性和实用性

Abstract: Cryptographic agility is a key prerequisite for maintaining the long-term security of digital communication, particularly in light of the transition to post-quantum cryptography. To systematically assess this capability, Hohm et al. proposed the Crypto Agility Maturity Model (CAMM).
  In this work, we present the first evaluation of the CAMM against established design principles for maturity models. Our analysis reveals that the CAMM only partially satisfies these principles: its scope and target groups remain ambiguous; acceptance criteria are insufficiently operationalized, limiting verifiability and replicability; and dependency relations exhibit redundancies, cycles, and omissions. Applying the CAMM to a simple real-world scenario further confirmed these issues, as several requirements at higher maturity levels proved inapplicable or unclear. Based on these findings, we propose concrete improvements to the CAMM to enable more consistent and reliable assessments of cryptographic agility.

</details>


### [76] [Scaling Exposes the Trigger: Input-Level Backdoor Detection in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Cross-Attention Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12446)
*Zida Li,Jun Li,Yuzhe Sha,Ziqiang Li,Lizhi Xiong,Zhangjie Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SET框架，通过主动扰动交叉注意力揭示CSRD现象，利用多尺度扰动构建响应偏移特征，学习良性响应空间，实现无需攻击先验知识的输入级后门检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有输入级后门检测方法依赖可观察异常，在隐蔽、语义保持的触发器设计下不可靠。现代后门攻击将触发器嵌入自然输入，需要更稳定、隐式、触发器无关的检测方法。

Method: 从主动探测角度出发，引入交叉注意力缩放扰动，发现CSRD现象。构建SET框架：对交叉注意力施加多尺度缩放扰动，构建响应偏移特征，使用少量干净样本学习紧凑的良性响应空间，通过偏离该空间的程度进行检测。

Result: SET在多样化攻击方法、触发器类型和模型设置中一致优于现有基线，在隐蔽隐式触发器场景下表现尤为突出。相比最佳基线，AUROC提升9.1%，ACC提升6.5%。

Conclusion: 通过主动扰动揭示的CSRD现象为后门检测提供了稳定、隐式的区分特征。SET框架无需攻击先验知识或模型训练访问权，具有实际部署的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in image synthesis, but their reliance on large-scale data and open ecosystems introduces serious backdoor security risks. Existing defenses, particularly input-level methods, are more practical for deployment but often rely on observable anomalies that become unreliable under stealthy, semantics-preserving trigger designs.
