Grounded in the Theory of Dual Channel Constraints (which states that code is a dual-channel medium combining an algorithmic (AL) channel for machine execution and a natural language (NL) channel for human communication, creating a unique safety-utility trade-off where a model must balance functional execution with responsible communication), we propose NLSafety-Utility Duality Score (SUDS), a metric that unifies code utility, safety adherence, and warning awareness into a single score across 12 ranked response scenarios, and Dual Reasoning (DR), a structured inference-time technique that requires an explicit safety audit and task-grounded code review before code generation. Evaluated on five LLMs across two benchmarks augmented with harmful keyword injections (820 and 2,135 samples), DR consistently achieves the highest SUDS across all models, improving mean SUDS by 1.32$\times$ to 3.42$\times$ over the baseline, while chain-of-thought prompting yields negligible safety gains and a safety-aware prompt provides only partial improvement. Further analysis reveals that DR's effectiveness scales with model capacity, that the one-shot exemplar primarily stabilizes output format for smaller models, and that structured reasoning cannot compensate for models with limited safety vocabularies.

</details>


### [149] [Automated BPMN Model Generation from Textual Process Descriptions: A Multi-Stage LLM-Driven Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12105)
*Ion Matei,Maksym Zhenirovskyy,Praveen Kumar Menaka Sekar,Hon Yung Wong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个基于LLM的多阶段流水线，用于从非结构化自然语言描述自动重建BPMN模型，包括自动化地构建基准真值数据并重建可执行的BPMN 2.0 XML图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非结构化自然语言描述自动重建BPMN模型面临挑战，包括异构建模规范、多语言来源以及缺乏可靠的基准真值数据。需要一种可扩展的解决方案来克服这些障碍。

Method: 开发了一个多阶段LLM驱动的流水线：1) 将多语言BPMN XML文件翻译成英语；2) 使用SpiffWorkflow进行面向执行的合规性检查；3) 通过有针对性的LLM引导修正迭代修复，生成一致的基准真值语料库；4) 从已验证模型中生成过程描述，用于重建可执行的BPMN 2.0 XML图；5) 引入结合结构度量、类型分布对齐和基于嵌入的语义度量的多维相似性框架。

Result: 在750个公共BPMN图的实证研究中，流水线生成了387个经过验证的基准真值模型，平均重建相似度超过0.75，包括约50个近乎完美的重建（仅存在微小命名差异）。

Conclusion: LLM能够大规模生成结构合规且语义有意义的BPMN图，证明了所提出流水线在自动化BPMN模型重建方面的有效性。

Abstract: Automatically reconstructing BPMN models from unstructured natural-language descriptions remains challenging due to heterogeneous modeling conventions, multilingual sources, and the lack of reliable ground truth. We present a scalable, multi-stage LLM-driven pipeline that automates both ground-truth construction and model reconstruction. Multilingual BPMN XML files are translated into English, validated using execution-oriented compliance checks in SpiffWorkflow, and iteratively repaired through targeted LLM-guided corrections to produce a consistent ground-truth corpus. From these validated models, process descriptions are generated and used to reconstruct executable BPMN~2.0 XML diagrams without manual curation. We introduce a multi-dimensional similarity framework combining structural metrics, type-distribution alignment, and embedding-based semantic measures. In an empirical study of 750 public BPMN diagrams, the pipeline generated 387 validated ground-truth models and achieved average reconstruction similarity above 0.75, including approximately 50 near-perfect reconstructions differing only in minor naming variations. The results demonstrate that LLMs can generate structurally compliant and semantically meaningful BPMN diagrams at scale.

</details>


### [150] [LLM-Based Automated Diagnosis Of Integration Test Failures At Google](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12108)
*Celal Ziftci,Ray Liu,Spencer Greene,Livio Dalloro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Auto-Diagnose：利用LLM诊断集成测试失败的AI工具，在Google内部部署中展现出90.14%的准确率，显著提升故障诊断效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成测试失败诊断面临三大挑战：日志量大、非结构化、异构性，导致认知负荷高、信噪比低、诊断耗时。开发者反映集成测试故障诊断时间远超单元测试，需要更高效的解决方案

Method: 提出Auto-Diagnose工具，利用LLM分析失败日志，生成简洁摘要并提取最相关日志行。工具集成到Google内部代码审查系统Critique中，提供上下文及时辅助

Result: 在71个真实故障的手动评估中达到90.14%的根因诊断准确率。Google范围内部署后，被用于52,635个不同失败测试。用户反馈显示仅5.8%案例被认为"无帮助"，在Critique的370个工具中帮助性排名第14。用户访谈确认了工具的实际效用

Conclusion: LLM凭借处理复杂文本数据的能力，在集成测试故障诊断中表现优异。将AI工具自动集成到开发者日常工作流受到积极认可，工具准确性是影响开发者感知和采用的关键因素

Abstract: Integration testing is critical for the quality and reliability of complex software systems. However, diagnosing their failures presents significant challenges due to the massive volume, unstructured nature, and heterogeneity of logs they generate. These result in a high cognitive load, low signal-to-noise ratio, and make diagnosis difficult and time-consuming. Developers complain about these difficulties consistently and report spending substantially more time diagnosing integration test failures compared to unit test failures. To address these shortcomings, we introduce Auto-Diagnose, a novel diagnosis tool that leverages LLMs to help developers efficiently determine the root cause of integration test failures. Auto-Diagnose analyzes failure logs, produces concise summaries with the most relevant log lines, and is integrated into Critique, Google's internal code review system, providing contextual and in-time assistance. Based on our case studies, Auto-Diagnose is highly effective. A manual evaluation conducted on 71 real-world failures demonstrated 90.14% accuracy in diagnosing the root cause. Following its Google-wide deployment, Auto-Diagnose was used across 52, 635 distinct failing tests. User feedback indicated that the tool was deemed "Not helpful" in only 5.8% of cases, and it was ranked #14 in helpfulness among 370 tools that post findings in Critique. Finally, user interviews confirmed the perceived usefulness of Auto-Diagnose and positive reception of integrating automatic diagnostic assistance into existing workflows. We conclude that LLMs are highly successful in diagnosing integration test failures due to their capacity to process and summarize complex textual data. Integrating such AI-powered tooling automatically into developers' daily workflows is perceived positively, with the tool's accuracy remaining a critical factor in shaping developer perception and adoption.

</details>


### [151] [Programming Language Co-Usage Patterns on Stack Overflow: Analysis of the Developer Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12123)
*Bachan Ghimire,Nitin Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析Stack Overflow上数十万开发者的行为数据，揭示了编程语言的实际使用模式、技术栈结构以及生态系统中的社区划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开发者如何在实际工作中组合使用编程语言，可以揭示软件生态系统的隐藏结构：哪些语言是互补使用的，哪些构成了连贯的技术栈，以及哪些语言连接了不同的开发社区。

Method: 采用三阶段实证分析流程：1) 从Stack Overflow数十万开发者涉及186种编程语言的帖子中挖掘数据；2) 应用FP-Growth频繁项集挖掘识别语言共现模式；3) 使用LDA主题建模分析开发者专业特征；4) 在加权共现图上应用Louvain社区检测发现宏观生态系统结构。

Result: FP-Growth识别出紧密耦合的语言集群（如shell/bash、Swift/Objective-C、C家族），其提升度远超个体语言流行度预测；LDA生成25个开发者专业画像（包括Apple平台开发者、科学/硬件程序员、函数式/学术程序员等）；Louvain将语言图划分为三个宏观社区：web/企业、Apple生态系统、系统/科学，并识别Java作为连接所有三个社区的最高度中心节点。

Conclusion: 三种方法独立地收敛于相同的生态系统结构，为研究结果提供了强有力的跨方法验证。该研究揭示了编程语言使用的实际模式、技术栈的连贯性以及生态系统中的社区划分，为理解软件生态系统的宏观结构提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Understanding how developers combine programming languages in practice reveals the hidden structure of the software ecosystem: which languages are used as complements, which define coherent technology stacks, and which bridge disparate communities. We present a three-phase empirical pipeline that mines Stack Overflow posts by hundreds of thousands of developers across 186 programming languages, applying FP-Growth frequent itemset mining, Latent Dirichlet Allocation topic modeling, and Louvain community detection on a weighted co-usage graph, with the goal of characterizing co-usage coupling, latent developer specializations, and macro-level ecosystem structure simultaneously from behavioral data. FP-Growth identifies tight coupling clusters such as shell/bash, Swift/Objective-C, and the C-family with lift values far exceeding what individual language popularity predicts. LDA produces 25 developer profiles including Apple-platform developers, scientific and hardware programmers, functional/academic programmers, and two distinct Unix scripting sub-profiles. Louvain partitions the language graph into three macro-communities: web/enterprise, Apple ecosystem, and systems/scientific, and identifies Java as the highest-degree hub connecting all three. All three methods independently converge on the same ecosystem structure, providing strong cross-method validation of the findings.

</details>


### [152] [Structural Anchors and Reasoning Fragility:Understanding CoT Robustness in LLM4Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12214)
*Yang Liu,Da Song,Armstrong Foundjem,Heng Li,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CoT提示在代码生成中的鲁棒性研究：发现CoT并非总是有益，其效果取决于扰动是否影响推理轨迹中的结构性敏感承诺点，并识别了三种轨迹变形模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要通过最终正确性评估CoT，缺乏对其在真实输入扰动下如何影响推理轨迹稳定性和内部不确定性动态的理解。需要探究CoT为何有时反而损害代码生成性能。

Method: 对6个模型和2个代码基准（MHPP和BigCodeBench）进行大规模实证研究，对任务文档字符串施加字符、单词和句子级别的系统扰动。通过token级不确定性记录完整生成轨迹，并定义三个结构性锚点：推理-代码转换、符号承诺和算法表达。

Result: 1) CoT不带来统一的性能或鲁棒性提升，其效果取决于模型家族、任务结构和提示明确性；2) CoT和No-CoT展现不同的鲁棒性特征，不同扰动家族触发不同失败模式；3) 识别三种重复出现的轨迹变形：延长、分支和简化；4) 早期不确定性可作为定位轨迹不稳定起始点的可靠诊断信号。

Conclusion: 研究为CoT在代码生成中的混合性能提供了统一解释，并提出了构建更鲁棒的基于推理的代码生成器的设计原则。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting is widely used to elicit explicit reasoning from large language models for code (LLM4Code). However, its impact on robustness and the stability of reasoning trajectories under realistic input perturbations remains poorly understood. Prior work has largely evaluated CoT through final correctness, leaving a critical gap in understanding how CoT reshapes internal uncertainty dynamics and why it sometimes harms rather than helps code generation. We suggest that CoT is not uniformly beneficial; instead, its robustness depends on whether perturbations destabilize structurally sensitive commitment points along the reasoning-to-code trajectory. We conduct a controlled, large-scale empirical study of CoT across six models and two code benchmarks (MHPP and BigCodeBench), subjecting task docstrings to systematic character-, word-, and sentence-level perturbations. We instrument full generation traces with token-level uncertainty and define three novel structural anchors: reasoning-code transition, symbolic commitment, and algorithmic articulation. Findings: (1) CoT does not yield uniform performance or robustness gains: its benefits are contingent on model family, task structure, and prompt explicitness. (2) CoT and No-CoT exhibit distinct robustness profiles, with different perturbation families triggering different failure modes. (3) We identify three recurrent trajectory deformations--Lengthening, Branching, and Simplification--that systematically emerge when perturbations interact with structural anchors and explain failure patterns. (4) Early-stage uncertainty serves as a reliable diagnostic signal for localizing where trajectory instability begins around sensitive anchors. These results provide a unified explanation for CoT's mixed performance and suggest design principles for building more robust reasoning-based code generators.

</details>


### [153] [Learning Project-wise Subsequent Code Edits via Interleaving Neural-based Induction and Tool-based Deduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12220)
*Chenyan Liu,Yun Lin,Yuhuan Huang,Jiaxin Chang,Binhang Qi,Bo Jiang,Zhiyong Huang,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TRACE是一个结合神经推理和工具推导的代码编辑解决方案，旨在平衡编辑范围、准确性和效率，通过交替使用语义编辑预测和语法编辑预测来提升项目级代码编辑任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM解决方案在项目级代码编辑任务中存在范围、准确性和效率之间的平衡问题。例如，Cursor在局部编辑范围准确率高但跨文件编辑表现下降，CoEdPilot在项目级编辑时存在效率限制。需要一种能同时优化这三个维度的解决方案。

Method: TRACE通过交替使用神经推理（用于语义编辑预测）和工具推导（用于语法编辑预测）来预测后续代码编辑。技术挑战包括：1）何时在神经推理和工具推导之间切换，通过训练神经模型检测何时调用IDE编辑工具；2）如何提升神经推理性能，通过提出细粒度的编辑表示来增强神经编辑模型。

Result: 论文未在摘要中提供具体实验结果，但声称TRACE能够推动编辑范围、准确性和效率的边界，通过结合神经推理和工具推导来优化项目级代码编辑任务。

Conclusion: TRACE提出了一种新颖的后续代码编辑解决方案，通过交替使用神经推理和工具推导来平衡编辑范围、准确性和效率，为项目级代码编辑任务提供了更优的AI支持方案。

Abstract: In industrial and open-source software engineering tasks, developers often perform project-wise code editing tasks, including feature enhancement, refactoring, and bug fixing, where the leading AI models are expected to support the productivity. Hence, researchers and practitioners have proposed and adopted many LLM-based solutions to facilitate their real-world development. However, they largely suffer from the balance among predicting scope, accuracy, and efficiency. For example, solutions like Cursor achieve high accuracy only in a local editing scope while its performance drops on cross-file edits. In contrast, solutions like CoEdPilot exhibit efficiency limitations when used to predict project-wise edits.
  In this work, we propose TRACE (Tool-integrated RecommendAtion for Code Editing), a novel subsequent code editing solution to push the boundary of scope, accuracy, and efficiency. Our rationale lies in that code edits are triggered for either semantic or syntactic reasons. Therefore, TRACE predicts subsequent edits by interleaving neural-based induction for semantic edit prediction and tool-based deduction for syntactic edit prediction. The tools can be any IDE facilities, such as refactoring tools (e.g., rename) or linting tools (e.g., use-def), providing decent performance of deducing edit-location and edit-generation. Technically, we address the challenge of (1) when to interleave between neural-based and tool-based prediction and (2) how to further improve the performance of neural-based prediction. As for the former, we learn a neural model to detect when to invoke IDE editing tools. As for the latter, we propose a novel and fine-grained editing representation to further boost the performance of neural editing models. ......

</details>


### [154] [CodeSpecBench: Benchmarking LLMs for Executable Behavioral Specification Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12268)
*Zaoyu Chen,Jianbo Dai,Boyu Zhu,Jingdong Wang,Huiming Wang,Xin Xu,Haoyang Yuan,Zhijiang Guo,Xiao-Ming Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeSpecBench是一个用于评估LLM生成可执行行为规范能力的基准测试，支持函数级和仓库级任务，通过执行评估协议验证规范的正确性和完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能够从自然语言生成代码，但它们对程序预期行为的理解程度尚不明确。现有的规范生成研究在评估方法、任务设置和规范表达能力方面存在局限，需要更全面的评估框架。

Method: 构建CodeSpecBench基准测试，包含从真实代码库中提取的函数级和仓库级任务，将规范编码为可执行的Python函数。采用执行评估协议，通过验证规范是否接受有效行为（正确性）和拒绝无效行为（完整性）来评估LLM性能。

Result: 评估15个最先进的LLM发现：在仓库级任务上性能急剧下降，最佳模型仅达到20.2%通过率；规范生成比代码生成更具挑战性，表明强大的编码性能不一定反映对程序语义的深刻理解。

Conclusion: CodeSpecBench为评估LLM生成可执行行为规范能力提供了全面框架，揭示了当前模型在理解程序语义方面的局限性，特别是在复杂仓库级任务上的表现不足。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate code from natural language, but the extent to which they capture intended program behavior remains unclear. Executable behavioral specifications, defined via preconditions and postconditions, provide a concrete means to assess such understanding. However, existing work on specification generation is constrained in evaluation methodology, task settings, and specification expressiveness. We introduce CodeSpecBench, a benchmark for executable behavioral specification generation under an execution-based evaluation protocol. CodeSpecBench supports both function-level and repository-level tasks and encodes specifications as executable Python functions. Constructed from diverse real-world codebases, it enables a realistic assessment of both correctness (accepting valid behaviors) and completeness (rejecting invalid behaviors). Evaluating 15 state-of-the-art LLMs on CodeSpecBench, we observe a sharp performance degradation on repository-level tasks, where the best model attains only a 20.2% pass rate. We further find that specification generation is substantially more challenging than code generation, indicating that strong coding performance does not necessarily reflect deep understanding of intended program semantics. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/SparksofAGI/CodeSpecBench.

</details>


### [155] [LLMs Are Not a Silver Bullet: A Case Study on Software Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12640)
*Xinyue Li,Sixuan Li,Ying Xiao,Jie M. Zhang,Zhou Yang,Xuanzhe Liu,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM-based bias mitigation方法在公平性和预测性能上均不如传统ML方法，现有LLM研究的积极结果主要源于不平衡测试数据的评估偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs的兴起，研究者开始探索其在偏差缓解中的应用，但尚不清楚LLM方法是否优于传统ML方法，缺乏对软件工程师的实际指导。

Method: 进行大规模研究，比较最先进的ML和LLM偏差缓解方法；分析现有LLM研究的评估设置；探索监督微调在完整训练集上的效果。

Result: ML方法在公平性和预测性能上始终优于LLM方法，即使强大的LLMs也无法超越已建立的ML基线；现有LLM研究的积极结果主要源于人为平衡的测试数据而非现实不平衡分布；监督微调虽能达到竞争性结果，但相对于传统ML方法的优势有限。

Conclusion: LLMs并非软件公平性的万能解决方案，传统ML方法在偏差缓解任务上仍具有优势，为软件工程师提供了明确的实践指导。

Abstract: Fairness is a critical requirement for human-related, high-stakes software systems, motivating extensive research on bias mitigation. Prior work has largely focused on tabular data settings using traditional Machine Learning (ML) methods. With the rapid rise of Large Language Models (LLMs), recent studies have begun to explore their use for bias mitigation in the same setting. However, it remains unclear whether LLM-based methods offer advantages over traditional ML methods, leaving software engineers without clear guidance for practical adoption. To address this gap, we present a large-scale study comparing state-of-the-art ML- and LLM-based bias mitigation methods. We find that ML-based methods consistently outperform LLM-based methods in both fairness and predictive performance, with even strong LLMs failing to surpass established ML baselines. To understand why prior LLM-based studies report favorable results, we analyze their evaluation settings and show that these gains are largely driven by artificially balanced test data rather than realistic imbalanced distributions. We further observe that existing LLM-based methods primarily rely on in-context learning and thus fail to leverage all available training data. Motivated by this, we explore supervised fine-tuning on the full training set and find that, while it achieves competitive results, its advantages over traditional ML methods remain limited. These findings suggest that LLMs are not a silver bullet for software fairness.

</details>


### [156] [Pricing-Driven Resource Allocation in the Computing Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12642)
*Alejandro García-Fernández,Boris Sedlak,José Antonio Parejo,Pantelis Frangoudis,Antonio Ruiz-Cortés,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用定价结构作为计算连续体中资源配置问题的通用表示方法，替代传统的特定拓扑表示，并开发了基于定价的工作流、合成数据生成和基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算连续体中的资源配置面临地理分布和异构环境的复杂性，现有方法使用特定基础设施拓扑表示，缺乏通用性。受SaaS生态系统定价结构的启发，探索定价作为配置空间的通用表示方法。

Method: 1) 提出基于定价的计算连续体资源配置问题形式化；2) 开发利用PRIME定价分析引擎的工作流，探索配置空间并计算满足约束的成本最优部署；3) 设计合成基础设施拓扑和工作负载需求生成过程；4) 创建包含9,600个预计算资源配置场景的数据集。

Result: 建立了定价作为配置空间通用表示的理论框架，开发了完整的工作流工具链，生成了大规模基准数据集，为计算连续体资源配置提供了新的解决方案。

Conclusion: 定价结构可以作为计算连续体中资源配置问题的通用表示方法，提供比传统特定拓扑表示更好的通用性和可扩展性，为异构分布式环境中的资源配置开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Deploying applications across the computing continuum requires selecting infrastructure nodes from geographically distributed and heterogeneous environments while satisfying constraints (e.g., performance, location). This decision problem is an important facet of resource allocation. As infrastructures grow in scale and heterogeneity, the resulting decision space becomes inherently combinatorial. Existing approaches typically formulate this problem as a constrained optimization task using ad-hoc representations of infrastructure topologies and demand, which hinders generalization across solutions. In contrast, Software as a Service ecosystems address a structurally similar configuration problem through pricings -structures whose plans and add-ons implicitly define the configuration space of possible subscriptions. Building on this observation, this work explores the potential of pricings as general-purpose representations of configuration spaces, positioning them as a promising alternative for addressing configuration problems, such as resource allocation, across the computing continuum. To this end, the paper presents the following contributions: i) a pricing-based formulation of the resource allocation problem in the computing continuum, enabling infrastructure configuration spaces to be represented using pricings; ii) a workflow that leverages PRIME, a pricing analysis engine, to explore these spaces and compute cost-optimal deployments satisfying functional and non-functional constraints; iii) generation processes for synthetic infrastructure topologies and workload demands; and iv) a dataset comprising 9,600 precomputed resource allocation scenarios to support benchmarking.

</details>


### [157] [Short Version of VERIFAI2026 Paper -- Learning Infused Formal Reasoning: Contract Synthesis, Artefact Reuse and Semantic Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12747)
*Arshad Beg,Diarmuid O'Donoghue,Rosemary Monahan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LIFR框架整合机器学习与形式验证，通过自动契约合成、验证工件的语义复用和数学基础理论，将验证转变为累积的知识驱动过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在自然语言处理、感知和决策任务中表现出色，但其行为往往不透明且难以验证，限制了在安全关键系统中的应用。形式方法虽然提供严格的数学机制，但形式规约的创建和维护劳动密集且难以扩展。

Method: 提出学习注入形式推理（LIFR）框架，整合机器学习技术与形式验证工作流，包括三个研究方向：1）从自然语言需求自动合成契约；2）使用图匹配和学习嵌入实现验证工件的语义复用；3）基于统一编程理论（UTP）和机构理论的数学基础。

Result: 论文提出了LIFR研究愿景，旨在将验证从孤立的正确性证明转变为累积的知识驱动过程，使规约、契约和证明能够在系统间合成、对齐和复用。

Conclusion: LIFR框架通过整合机器学习与形式验证，有望解决形式规约创建和维护的扩展性问题，使验证成为可扩展、知识驱动的过程，促进AI系统在安全关键领域的应用。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems have achieved remarkable capability in natural language processing, perception and decision-making tasks. However, their behaviour often remains opaque and difficult to verify, limiting their applicability in safety-critical systems. Formal methods provide mathematically rigorous mechanisms for specifying and verifying system behaviour, yet the creation and maintenance of formal specifications remains labour intensive and difficult to scale. This paper outlines a research vision called Learning-Infused Formal Reasoning (LIFR), which integrates machine learning techniques with formal verification workflows. The framework focuses on three complementary research directions: automated contract synthesis from natural language requirements, semantic reuse of verification artifacts using graph matching and learning-based embeddings, and mathematically grounded semantic foundations based on the Unifying Theories of Programming (UTP) and the Theory of Institutions. Together these research threads aim to transform verification from isolated correctness proofs into a cumulative knowledge-driven process where specifications, contracts and proofs can be synthesised, aligned and reused across systems.

</details>


### [158] [Evaluating LLMs Code Reasoning Under Real-World Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12881)
*Changshu Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: R2Eval是一个包含135个代码推理问题的基准测试，从10个广泛使用的Python项目中提取，通过序列化复合和自定义类型来保留真实世界数据复杂性，更真实地评估LLMs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码推理基准测试大多依赖简化的LLM生成代码片段或人工编写的解决方案，输入输出通常限制为基本类型，无法反映真实项目的结构和依赖关系，限制了评估实际泛化能力。

Method: 从10个广泛使用的Python项目中提取135个代码推理问题，通过序列化复合和自定义类型来保留真实世界数据复杂性，构建更真实的评估基准。

Result: 提出了R2Eval基准测试，包含135个真实世界代码推理问题，能够更准确地评估LLMs在实际项目中的代码推理能力。

Conclusion: R2Eval通过从真实项目中提取问题并保留复杂数据类型，提供了更贴近实际开发场景的代码推理评估基准，有助于更准确地衡量LLMs的实用泛化能力。

Abstract: Code reasoning tasks are increasingly crucial to evaluating large language models (LLMs). Yet most existing benchmarks rely on simplistic, LLM-generated snippets or human-written solutions to code challenges and often restrict inputs and outputs to primitive types, failing to reflect the structure and dependencies of real-world projects. These simplifications limit their ability to measure practical generalizability. We present R2Eval1, a benchmark of 135 code reasoning problems drawn from ten widely used Python projects. Unlike prior work, R2Eval serializes compound and custom types, preserving real-world data complexity and enabling a more realistic assessment of LLMs.

</details>


### [159] [Personalizing LLM-Based Conversational Programming Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12998)
*Jonan Richards*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究LLM对话编程助手的个性化需求，以应对开发者认知和组织背景多样性带来的挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为对话式编程助手在软件工程中展现出潜力，但开发者需求的多样性、对话交互的模糊性和无边界性使得有效满足这些需求变得复杂。需要研究认知和组织背景多样性如何影响开发者需求，以提升LLM助手的包容性。

Method: 通过当前和未来的工作来表征认知和组织背景多样性对开发者需求的影响，探索个性化作为提高LLM对话编程助手包容性的手段。

Result: 论文讨论了正在进行和未来的研究工作，尚未提供具体实验结果，但提出了通过个性化解决LLM助手包容性问题的研究方向。

Conclusion: 需要关注开发者认知和组织背景的多样性，通过个性化方法提高LLM对话编程助手的包容性，以更好地满足不同开发者的需求。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown much promise in powering a variety of software engineering (SE) tools. Offering natural language as an intuitive interaction mechanism, LLMs have recently been employed as conversational ``programming assistants'' capable of supporting several SE activities simultaneously. As with any SE tool, it is crucial that these assistants effectively meet developers' needs. Recent studies have shown addressing this challenge is complicated by the variety in developers' needs, and the ambiguous and unbounded nature of conversational interaction. This paper discusses our current and future work towards characterizing how diversity in cognition and organizational context impacts developers' needs, and exploring personalization as a means of improving the inclusivity of LLM-based conversational programming assistants.

</details>
